# G4-04 «İş yükü» Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking. **Task 1 runs first, alone** (the permission keys both halves read). Then two implementers run the **engine lane** (Tasks 2–5) and the **dashboard lane** (Tasks 6–12) **in parallel, in the same worktree**. Task 13 (docs) and Task 14 (gates) run after both lanes report.

**Goal:** A page `/is-yuku` «İş yükü» for `workload.view` or `shifts.manage` that shows, per unit and for the current shift, the patients, the nurses assigned, patients per nurse, the unassigned patients, the late and the remaining work and the alarms waiting — counts only, nurse cards alphabetical with beds, patients and remaining work only — and, for `shifts.manage`, «Nöbet ataması»: the charge nurse assigns nurses to the unit's beds for this shift or the next, by the window's own key, audited, one active nurse per bed per window.

**Architecture:**
- **Engine:**
  - the key `workload.view` (the 30th), held by the default `charge_nurse` group (migration `0022`) and by the system group by rule; `shifts.manage` says what it does now;
  - one wall-clock window helper, `api/shiftclock.py` `shift_windows` (local starts, a candidate search; `fluid_day` untouched);
  - a new router module `admission/shifts.py` (prefix `/shift-schedule`): `GET` (either key) answers the window and the units in reach with their active rows (a row's unit is its bed's) and the other windows' overlapping rows; `GET /nurses` the people the form offers, each with whether her scope reaches the unit; `PUT` saves one unit's window by its key (`shift_start`; 409 `shift_changed`), `expected` required (409), under the unit's row lock, ends flushed with `closed_at` before inserts, a coded 409 at the index, audited `shift.assign`. The unused `POST`, its service function and the S-25 warning are retired;
  - migration `0022`: the key, `closed_at`, the rows of a wrong unit and the duplicate active rows ended, a partial unique index `(bed_id, shift_start) WHERE is_active` (also declared on the model).
- **Dashboard:**
  - G4-02's poll (`useSummaryReads`) gains one optional extra read in the same round: the page reads the tree and `/scope/me` there, and in `extra` `GET /shift-schedule?shift=current` and then `/tasks` over the week ending with that shift, every 60 s while visible;
  - a pure `screens/isyuku/isyuku.js` joins structure × assignment × tasks × live board into rows, nurse cards and unassigned beds — each number real or «—» with its reason (Decision 18) — and builds the form's model and body;
  - `IsYukuScreen.jsx` words it, with «Nöbet ataması» (its own reads of one unit's window and the offered people);
  - the route after `kalite`, a work screen for either key; `shift.assign` gets its «Denetim bütünlüğü» label and the two new refusal codes their words.

**Tech Stack:**
- Engine: Python 3.14, FastAPI, SQLAlchemy 2 (async), Alembic, pytest.
- Dashboard: React 18, Vitest 2, Testing Library, Tailwind.
- Guide: Turkish Markdown.

**Spec (the contract):** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-12-g4-is-yuku-design.md` — committed at `439bb4e0`, amended at `fe3c1af6` (§D8: the downgrade re-activates nothing) and at `bef46919` (**§5 Amendments, A1–A13 and M1–M9, which supersede D3, D4, D6, D7 and D8 where they differ**). The branch `feat/g4-is-yuku` was cut from main `c9a3c9fd` and took main `746f30a9` at `ecfff9c3` (`/alarm-quality` on a worker thread; the i18n tables in two chunks of their own, `dashboard/vite.config.js` `manualChunks`); this plan is written for head `ecfff9c3`. Read the spec whole — §5 last — before your first task.

---

## Plan notes: where the spec meets the code

Each note records a choice this plan makes where the spec and the tree disagree, or where the spec leaves a detail open. The notes bind the implementers. A reviewer who disagrees raises it; the implementer does not improvise.

**N1: The shift windows — one wall-clock helper (§5 A2).**
- New module `homunculus/api/shiftclock.py`: `Shift(start, end, which)` and `shift_windows(now, zone, start_a, start_b) → (current, next)`. The starts are Görevlerim's `shift_start_a` / `shift_start_b` (whole hours, tenant-local, never equal, not necessarily 12 apart).
- The rule: the candidate starts are the local `start_a` ("A") and `start_b` ("B") o'clock of the four local days from the day before `now`'s to two days after, each turned into its UTC instant and sorted; the current window runs from the latest start at or before `now` to the next start; «next» from there to the one after. A boundary instant begins the new window; a window that crosses midnight belongs to the day it began; with 08/16 the windows are 8 and 16 hours. A window's **key** is the UTC instant of its local start (`nurse_bed_assignments.shift_start`).
- **Daylight saving:** a window is bounded by the local wall clock, so the windows tile time — B is 13 hours the night the clock goes back, 11 the night it goes forward. `api/fluids.py` `fluid_day` (G3-03) adds 12 and 24 hours to a UTC instant and is **not changed**; on those two nights its second window and the shift B differ by an hour (Baku and Istanbul keep no summer time): the guide records it (Task 13), and Task 2's test pins it. tzdata is not installed on the test host (`ZoneInfo("Europe/Berlin")` fails there), so the test writes its own summer-time zone (`_Summer`, PEP 495 `fold`).
- `api/tasks.py` `shift_window` (Görevlerim's list window: 24 hours from the latest start) is a different window and stays as it is.

**N2: Which window, from which settings (§5 A1, M4).**
- The starts and the zone are read from the app's task store, `request.app.state.tasks` — `settings(slug) or DEFAULT_SETTINGS` and `zone_of(timezone(slug))` — exactly as `GET /tasks`, `/pharmacy` and `/patients/{id}/fluids` read them; no second reader of `task_settings`. An app without a store reads the defaults (08, 20, UTC). In the DB tests the store is the in-memory one (UTC); a test fixes the clock with `monkeypatch.setattr(shifts, "_now", …)`.
- `GET ?shift=current|next` (default `current`) answers that window. The **PUT** names its window by its key, `shift_start` (the GET answer's `shift.start`, an aware ISO instant): the server computes the current and the next window at save time and takes the one whose start equals it; neither is **409 `shift_changed`** (a coded refusal), nothing written. A form read at 19:50 and saved at 20:00:30 thus never writes into the window after the one it showed (Task 3 tests it at 20:00:30: the 08:00 key is refused, the 20:00 key — «next» at 19:50, current now — is saved).
- A row belongs to the window whose key equals its `shift_start`. If an administrator moves the hours, earlier rows match no window: N13's `other_rows` and the PUT's ending of them (§5 A3).

**N3: Where the routes live; the POST retired.**
- A new module `homunculus/admission/shifts.py` (the router `router`, prefix `/shift-schedule`): `admission/router.py` is 1 700 lines, and its `sched_router` section is removed, not extended. `api/app.py` imports the new router under the old name `shift_schedule_router`; its `include_router` line stays.
- `tests/test_route_permissions.py`: `GUARDED_MODULES` gains `homunculus.admission.shifts`; `EXPECTED` loses `("POST", "/shift-schedule")` and gains three entries — **76 → 78**. `BOARD_EXPECTED` (`tests/test_board_permissions.py`, `homunculus.api.app` routes only) does not change.
- The POST's remains go: `service.create_shift_schedule` (its only caller), the four schemas (`NurseBedAssignmentCreate`, `ShiftScheduleCreate`, `NurseBedAssignmentResponse`, `ShiftScheduleResponse`), `service.py`'s now-unused imports of `NurseBedAssignment` and `Unit`, the docstring lines of both modules, and the S-25 beds-per-nurse warning altogether (§5 A12).
- Existing tests: `tests/test_admission_workflow.py::test_shift_schedule_soft_overcapacity_warning` is deleted with its function and S-25 (a comment says where the assignment is tested now). `tests/test_tenant_isolation.py`'s three POST cases are rewritten onto the PUT (control 200; the body names the NEXT window of the default starts in UTC, so a shift change during the call does not refuse it) and its `BY_ID` gains the two GETs by `unit_id`. `tests/test_admission_api.py` and `tests/test_structure_api.py` insert one `NurseBedAssignment` row each through the ORM (lock tests): unchanged, the index allows them. A POST to the path now answers 405 (tested).

**N4: A row's unit is its BED's unit (§5 A8).** Every read and the PUT's own select rows through `bed → room → unit`, never the row's `unit_id` column (the retired POST checked beds against the tenant only). The PUT writes `unit_id` = the unit asked for, and takes only that unit's beds, so the two agree from now on; migration 0022 ends the active rows that disagree.

**N5: Reach.**
- `GET /shift-schedule` lists the caller tenant's units (through `Site.tenant_id`) filtered by `unitscope.unit_clause` — her **unit** grants; unrestricted: every unit — by name, then id, closed units included (the page shows open ones only). A unit with no row is listed with `items: []`.
- With `unit_id` (the form): another tenant's or a missing unit is the 404 `Unit not found.`; a unit outside her units the **403 `out_of_scope`** (404 → 403, §5 M3). `GET /nurses` and `PUT` check the unit the same way. A bed-only grant reaches no unit (D4); a scope that cannot be read reaches nothing.

**N6: The PUT, in order (§5 A1, A7, A10, M3) — every check before anything is written.**
1. The body `{unit_id, shift_start, items: [{bed_id, nurse_id}], expected: [{bed_id, nurse_id}]}`, unknown keys refused (`extra="forbid"`), **`expected` required** (an empty list means «none held»): FastAPI's own 422.
2. `shifts.manage` (403).
3. **404**: the unit and every bed are the caller's tenant's (`Unit not found.` / `Bed not found.`).
4. **403**: the unit is one of her units (`out_of_scope`).
5. **422**: a bed named twice (`field_invalid:items`); the person of every pair that is NOT in `expected` is an active member of the tenant (`Kullanıcı bu kurumun üyesi değil`, `scope.ensure_members`; a kept pair skips it — step 8 proves it held).
6. **409 `shift_changed`**: `shift_start` is neither the current nor the next window's key (N2).
7. **The lock** (N7), then the unit's active rows of that window (`held`) and of the windows overlapping it (`others`) are read again.
8. **409** `CONCURRENT_CHANGE` («Kayıt aynı anda değiştirildi; lütfen tekrar deneyin», the dashboard's `concurrentChange`, as PUT /scope/{user_id}) when `expected` ≠ `held` as sets; rolled back, nothing audited.
9. **422 `field_invalid:items`** for a NEW pair (one not in `held`) whose bed is not an open bed of that unit (the bed, its room and the unit `is_active`), or whose person is not offered (N8). A pair already held is kept as it is, whatever has happened to its bed or its person since (§5 A10: «artık atanamaz» keeps her bed).
10. The write: the held rows the body leaves out, and every `others` row, `is_active = false, closed_at = now` — **executed before the inserts** (the partial unique index cannot be deferred); a kept pair's row is not touched; one new row per new pair (`charge_nurse_id` = the caller, the window's bounds); flush. An `IntegrityError` there is the coded **409 `assignment_conflict`**, never a 500.
11. The soft warnings (N9), the commit, the audit row (N10), and the answer: the unit's new state as `GET` gives it, plus `warnings`.

**N7: The per-unit lock.** The unit's row, `SELECT … FOR NO KEY UPDATE` (`with_for_update(key_share=True)`, `populate_existing`) — the statement of `tenant/router.py` `_lock_unit`, not an advisory lock: every structure write inside a unit takes this lock first, so a PUT and a bed close or relabel in that unit run one after the other (the open-bed check holds until the commit), and two PUTs of one unit run one after the other (the second re-reads the first's rows and is refused by `expected`; Task 3 tests two saves at once). The beds are not locked one by one: a relabel takes the unit's lock first (`patch_bed`). The inserts take KEY SHARE on the unit, the beds and the users, which FOR NO KEY UPDATE lets through; nothing waits on a lock while holding a bed, so no deadlock with an admission (bed lock, then a KEY SHARE on the unit).

**N8: Who is offered (§5 A10, A11).**
- `admission/service.py` gains `NURSE_GROUPS = ("nurse", "charge_nurse")` and `nurse_ids(tenant_id)` beside `PHYSICIAN_GROUPS` / `physician_ids`, built the same way: an active user, an active membership in the tenant, a group of that tenant whose slug is in `NURSE_GROUPS`. Not `rapid_response` (Görevlerim's `NURSE_SLUGS` has it): that team is not assigned to ward beds. A hospital's own nurse group is not offered (D7).
- `GET /shift-schedule/nurses?unit_id=` answers `[{id, name, username, reaches_unit}]`: `name` the full name, else the user name; `reaches_unit` whether her own scope (`unitscope.caller_scope` → `reaches(unit)`) reaches the unit asked for — read person by person; sorted by the case-folded name, then the id. The `unit_id` is required and checked (N5); it filters nobody.
- On the page: one whose scope does not reach the unit is marked «bu birimi göremez» in the picker; a person who holds a row of the window but is no longer offered appears only in the picker of the bed she holds, marked «artık atanamaz». When two people of one list share a name, the user name is added in brackets (the picker and the cards); one order everywhere: the interface language's `localeCompare`, then the id.

**N9: Warnings.** No beds-per-nurse warning (§5 A12; `max_beds_per_nurse` is a sourceless code default). The PUT answers `warnings: [{code: "unit_not_reached", nurse_id}]` — one per distinct nurse of the saved items whose scope does not reach the unit, by id — and saves anyway; the form words each with her name: «Nigar Sentetik bu birimi göremez: kendi panosunda bu yatakların hastaları görünmez (uyarı, engel değil).»

**N10: The audit row.** `trail.record(request, hospital, "shift.assign", subject=<unit id>, payload=…)` after the commit (§5 M8: the subject is the unit), `patient_id` empty: `{unit_id, shift_start, shift_end` (ISO, `+00:00`)`, before` (the held pairs)`, after` (the body's pairs)`, other` (the overlapping rows of other windows it ended)`, actor` (the user name)`}`; pairs as `[bed_id, nurse_id]`, sorted. Written on every successful PUT. `trail.LOGGED_KEYS` already carries `unit_id`. The dashboard's `engineTypes.test.js` reads the type from the engine's text and fails until `/denetim` names it (Task 11).

**N11: nginx, the dev server, the access log.** `/shift-schedule` is already proxied (nginx's API `location` regex; `dashboard/vite.config.js`'s list, unchanged by main's `manualChunks`). `/shift-schedule/nurses` has no path parameter and `shift-schedule` is no keyed prefix of the access-log map's catch-all, so `default $uri` logs it as it is; a query string is never logged (`tests/test_access_log_redaction.py` walks the app's routes, the new ones included). **No change to `deploy/local/web/nginx.conf` or `dashboard/vite.config.js`**; should one ever be needed (a merge, N19), `vite.config.js` is the dashboard lane's and `nginx.conf` the engine lane's (the Order table). `tests/test_nginx_routes.py`'s `API_PATHS` gains `/shift-schedule/nurses`.

**N12: The migration `0022_workload_view` (spec D8, §5 A8, M1, M2).**
- `down_revision = "0020"` (main's head). **If G3-05's `0021_vital_readings` reaches main first, it becomes `"0021"`** — one line, and the tuple in `test_the_migration_names_the_group_and_the_index_the_code_names`; whoever merges second makes the change; `tests/test_audit_postgres.py`'s single-head test holds it. The migration test never pins the head (`>= "0022"`).
- Upgrade: (1) `workload.view` for every `charge_nurse` group, `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`; (2) `closed_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL`; (3) an active row whose `unit_id` is not its bed's unit is ended:
  `UPDATE nurse_bed_assignments AS a SET is_active = false, closed_at = now() FROM beds AS b JOIN rooms AS r ON r.id = b.room_id WHERE b.id = a.bed_id AND a.is_active AND a.unit_id <> r.unit_id`;
  (4) of the remaining active rows of one `(bed_id, shift_start)` the newest by `created_at`, then `id`, stays:
  `UPDATE nurse_bed_assignments SET is_active = false, closed_at = now() WHERE id IN (SELECT id FROM (SELECT id, row_number() OVER (PARTITION BY bed_id, shift_start ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC) AS n FROM nurse_bed_assignments WHERE is_active) AS ranked WHERE n > 1)`;
  (5) the partial unique index **`uq_nurse_bed_active_shift`** ON `(bed_id, shift_start)` WHERE `is_active`.
- The model declares the column and the same index (`NURSE_BED_ACTIVE_INDEX`, `postgresql_where` and `sqlite_where`, as `CareTeamMember`), so `create_all` and dev SQLite hold them too; `tests/test_migration_0010_schema_parity.py` compares the column's type.
- The downgrade drops the index, the column (the end times go with it) and the key from every group; it **re-activates no row**. The docstring names the old image's exception (an old api's POST could insert a second active row after 0022; no client calls it).

**N13: The page's numbers and their sources (§5 A4–A6, A13, M6, M9).** Rows: the tree's **open** units (a closed unit has no open bed). Reach = `GET /scope/me` (`ozet.js` `reachSource`); «listed» = the unit is in the GET answer.

| Number | Source(s) | «—» and why, never 0, when |
|---|---|---|
| Bu nöbet · HH:MM–HH:MM (header) | GET /shift-schedule | not read / failed / refused; its shift has ended («nöbet değişti, yenileniyor») |
| Hasta | `/tenant/structure`: open beds in OCCUPIED, PENDING_DISCHARGE, PENDING_TRANSFER_OUT | the tree's read is not fresh; the unit is out of reach and the tree is not the whole hospital's («yetki yok»); the reach is not known and the tree is not whole |
| Hemşire | GET /shift-schedule: distinct nurses of the unit's rows on its **open** beds; none → «Bu nöbet için atama yok» (only from a fresh answer that lists the unit) | the GET is missing or stale; the unit is not listed («yetki yok») |
| Hemşire başına hasta | the occupied beds with a row ÷ the nurses: «{n} hasta / {m} hemşire · ortalama {x}» (one decimal; comma in tr/az/ru, point in en); no nurse → «—» | the Hemşire cell's reasons, then Hasta's |
| Atanmamış hasta | the occupied beds without a row (counted when the unit has no assignment too) | as Hemşire başına hasta |
| Geciken iş | `/tasks` over [shift end − 7 days, shift end): open hand tasks the server marks `late` + a vital task in state `late` (`ozet.js` `lateOf`), by the card's `unit_id` | the task read is refused (no `tasks.view`: «yetki yok») / failed / of another shape (a hand task without `state` or `due_at`); the GET is missing or stale (the read hangs on its window); the unit is out of reach |
| Kalan iş | the same read: open hand tasks **not** late with `due_at` < shift end; no vital task | as Geciken iş |
| Yanıt bekleyen alarm | the live board's `needsAnswer` by the card's unit (`ozet.js` `boardSource`), LIVE only | the board is connecting («okunuyor…»), DEGRADED («pano güncel değil»), unavailable, not readable (no `board.view`); the unit is out of reach |
| Nurse card · Yataklar | her rows on the selection's open beds, by their structure name | the GET is missing or stale (the whole cards block says why) |
| Nurse card · Hasta | her occupied beds | the tree's read is not fresh |
| Nurse card · Kalan iş | her beds → the live board's cards on them (`bedId`) → those patients' «Kalan iş» in the task read (§5 A5: `/tasks` names no bed) | the task read's reasons; then the board not LIVE |
| Atanmamış (group) | the selection's occupied beds without a row | the tree's read is not fresh |

A nurse card has no late count and no alarm count (§5 A13; owner «Hayır, yalnız birimde»). The structure's occupancy and the board's `bedId` can disagree for a patient the HIS bed map placed (§5 M9): a card may then read «0» patients while its tasks come from the board, as on G4-02 — recorded, not fixed.

**N14: The reads.**
- `useSummaryReads` gains an optional `extra = {may, read(api), valid}` — one more read in the same round, on the same 60 s visible-only clock, counted in «HH:MM itibarıyla»'s full round, shape-checked like the others. The page passes its rights as `{structure, tasks: false, pending: false}` (it reads neither G4-02's `/tasks` nor the handovers) and `extra = {may: workload.view or shifts.manage, read: readWorkAndTasks | readWorkAlone, valid: isWorkAnswer}`.
- `readWork(api, mayTasks)`: `GET /shift-schedule?shift=current`, then — with `tasks.view` — `GET /tasks?from=<shift.end − 7 days>&to=<shift.end>` (the widest range `/tasks` takes, §5 A4). A failed task read keeps the schedule (its cells say why); a session that ended is re-thrown.
- `workSources(read, rights, now)`: a schedule whose `shift.end` ≤ now is SHIFT_CHANGED until the next read, and so is the task read over it (§5 A6). The screen passes `Date.now()`.
- The form reads its own two answers — `GET /shift-schedule?shift=…&unit_id=…` and `GET /shift-schedule/nurses?unit_id=…` — on open, on a change of window, after a save, after a 409 and on «Yeniden dene»; it is drawn only when both and the structure's read have succeeded (§5 A6). Its `expected` is every row its read returned — a closed bed's and a no-longer-offered nurse's included — taken once; the poll does not rebuild a form being edited (§5 A7). Its `items` are the pickers with a nurse, so a row on a closed bed is ended by the next save.

**N15: The page.**
- Header: «İş yükü»; «Bu nöbet · 08:00–20:00» in the browser's clock (`hhmm`; «Bu nöbet — {neden}» without a fresh answer); «HH:MM itibarıyla» of the last full round (`ozet.asOf`); «Sayım, puan değil · kişiler sıralanmaz»; «Yenile» (`ozet.refresh`). Under the table, when a listed unit has `other_rows` > 0: «Nöbet saatleri değişti; bu nöbetin ataması yeniden yapılmalı.» (§5 A3; the form says it too for its window).
- The unit picker is a `<select>` labelled «Birim» above the nurse cards: «Tüm birimler», then the units the GET lists; a choice the rows no longer hold falls back to «Tüm birimler». The form needs one unit: with «Tüm birimler» it says «Atama için yukarıdan bir birim seçin.»
- «Nöbet ataması · {birim}»: «Bu nöbet» / «Sonraki nöbet» (a `Seg` in the box's header), the window's hours, one `<select>` per open bed (labelled by its structure name; «— atanmadı —» first), «Kaydet». After a save: «Atama kaydedildi.» and the soft warnings; a 409 `shift_changed`: «Nöbet bu arada değişti — liste yenilendi; yeni nöbete yeniden bakın.»; any other 409: «Bu arada başka biri değiştirdi — liste yenilendi; yeniden bakın.» — both read the form and the page again; another refusal: «Kaydedilmedi: {neden}» (`readErrorText`), the choices kept (§5 A6).
- Icon `users` (the prototype's own for «İş yükü», `tasarim-taslaklari/app/shell.js`; used by no route). Route `{ id: "isyuku", path: "/is-yuku", icon: "users", labelKey: "isyuku.nav", shortKey: "isyuku.navShort", perms: ["workload.view", "shifts.manage"], work: true, nav: "main" }` (§5 M6), after `kalite`, before `settings`. `routes.test.js`'s one pinned tail moves to the new block: `slice(-5)` = `["eczane", "ozet", "kalite", "isyuku", "settings"]`; the kalite block asserts only that `isyuku` follows it. A group given either key alone signs in to it and lands on it; the charge nurse's home stays «Yataklar».

**N16: The words.** A namespace `isyuku` (tr, az, ru, en). «İş yükü» is the prototype's Azerbaijani too (`birim.js` `m_workload`): `isyuku.nav`, `isyuku.navShort`, `isyuku.title` go into `AZ_SAME_AS_TR`; every Russian text has a Cyrillic word, so `NEUTRAL` gains nothing. Russian «Нагрузка», English «Workload» — unique short rail labels in each language. This page's own reason is `isyuku.why.shiftChanged`; the two coded refusals' texts are `isyuku.err.shiftChanged` and `isyuku.err.conflict` (the `<namespace>.err.*` rule `apiErrors.test.js` pins). Reused: `ozet.why.*`, `ozet.asOf`, `ozet.noFullRead`, `ozet.refresh`, `admin.retry`. `shifts.manage`'s label changes in four languages with its engine text (§5 M5).

**N17: Two new refusal codes (§5 M3).** `admission/errors.py` gains `SHIFT_CHANGED = "shift_changed"` and `ASSIGNMENT_CONFLICT = "assignment_conflict"` in a block of their own after B1c's (`CODES` widened the same way); `tests/test_admission_form.py`'s set of codes gains them (engine, Task 3). `dashboard/src/live/apiErrors.js` `ADMISSION_CODE_KEYS` maps them to `isyuku.err.*`; `apiErrors.test.js`'s count goes 33 → 35 and its namespace rule gains `isyuku` (dashboard, Task 11). A `field_invalid:items` is read as the generic field sentence (`kabul.invalid.other`): the form offers only open beds and offered people, so it meets one only in a race.

**N18: The cross-lane reds.** Both lanes work in one worktree; the dashboard's drift tests read the engine's files as text. Measured in the dry run:
- after Task 1 alone, until Task 6: `catalogue.test.js` and `admin.testutil.test.js` (the Order above);
- after Task 3, with the dashboard lane not yet at Task 11 (`5 failed`): `engineTypes.test.js` «names every type the engine writes» (`shift.assign`); `apiErrors.test.js` «the readers found the codes and the fields the engine raises» (35, not 33), «the map holds every code errors.py raises, and nothing else», and the two tests it draws per code from `errors.py`, «shift_changed has a text in the four languages» and «assignment_conflict has a text in the four languages»;
- after Task 11 with Task 3 not yet in (`3 failed`): `engineTypes.test.js` «names no type the engine does not write» (`shift.assign`), `apiErrors.test.js` «the readers found the codes…» (33, not 35) and «the map holds every code…» the other way;

## Execution rules (both lanes; read before Task 0)

- **Code exploration:**
  - `graphify-out/graph.json` lives in the main checkout `C:/Users/User/Desktop/Project/Homunculus AI` and was built from an older branch.
  - Before your first grep or source read for a codebase question, run `graphify query "<question>"` from that directory: `cd "/c/Users/User/Desktop/Project/Homunculus AI" && graphify query "..."`. Then read the **worktree's** files (`C:\tmp\g4-isyuku`) for the current lines.
  - Use `graphify path "<A>" "<B>"` or `graphify explain "<concept>"` for focused questions.
  - **Never run `graphify update`.**
- **Tests run in the FOREGROUND only.**
  - Never background a test run and wait on it. Split a long run into chunks instead (the gates do).
  - Report before you wait on anything.
- **Engine tests** run from `C:\tmp\g4-isyuku\homunculus-engine`, always written out in full (shell variables do not persist between tool calls):
  `"/c/Users/User/Desktop/Project/Homunculus AI/homunculus-engine/.venv/Scripts/python.exe" -m pytest <paths> -q -p no:cacheprovider`
  pytest's `pythonpath = ["src", "tests"]` (pyproject) puts the worktree's `src` ahead of the venv's editable install of the main checkout; Task 0 checks it.
- **Dashboard tests** run from `C:\tmp\g4-isyuku\dashboard`: `npx vitest run <files>`. `node_modules` is a junction and is already present. Two vitest runs at once on the shared junction (another session's included) can fail with «Vitest failed to find the current suite»: run again, it is not a code error.
- **Never:**
  - `npm install` or `pip install`;
  - set `DATABASE_URL`. DB tests use `TEST_DATABASE_URL`, and only on the throwaway Postgres below;
  - run `tests/test_infinity_vm_pilot.py`.
- **Git:**
  - Never a bare `git stash`. Never `git add -A`, never `git commit -a`.
  - Every commit names its paths, run from `C:\tmp\g4-isyuku`:
    `git add <paths>`, then `git commit -m "<subject>" -m "Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>" -- <paths>`.
    The pathspec matters: the other lane may have files staged at the same moment, and `git commit -- <paths>` commits only yours. A new file must be `git add`ed before the pathspec commit can name it.
  - If `.git/index.lock` exists, the other lane is committing: wait a few seconds and retry. **Never delete it.**
- **Line endings and editing:** `core.autocrlf=true`, so tracked files are CRLF in the working tree.
  - Edit existing files with the **Edit** tool, or with a `python -I -X utf8` script that writes `\r\n`.
  - Create new files with the **Write** tool.
  - **Never** write code with a Bash heredoc: the Bash tool mangles backslashes in heredocs.
- **Find blocks:** every **find** block below occurs **exactly once** in its file at that point of the plan (checked on a scratch copy of `ecfff9c3` when the plan was written, by applying the plan's own blocks in order). If one does not match, stop and report; do not guess.
- **No patient data:** synthetic ids only (`S-101`, `202600000001`, `192.0.2.x`), synthetic names (`Ayşe Sentetik`, `Leyla Sentetik`, `Sentetik Hasta`).
- Do not push. Do not merge. Do not touch the VM.

### The throwaway Postgres (DB-backed tests: Tasks 3, 4, 5 and 14)

The DB tests drop every table, so they run only against a private, disposable database. **Never use the shared `56432`**, nor another session's container. Each line is one foreground tool call, from `C:\tmp\g4-isyuku\homunculus-engine`:

```bash
docker run -d --rm --name isyuku-pg -p 127.0.0.1:56457:5432 -e POSTGRES_PASSWORD=ozet -e POSTGRES_DB=ozet postgres:16
timeout 60 bash -c 'until docker exec isyuku-pg createdb -U postgres ozet_test 2>/dev/null; do :; done'
TEST_DATABASE_URL=postgresql+asyncpg://postgres:ozet@127.0.0.1:56457/ozet_test "/c/Users/User/Desktop/Project/Homunculus AI/homunculus-engine/.venv/Scripts/python.exe" -m pytest <paths> -q -p no:cacheprovider
docker stop isyuku-pg
```

- `pg_isready` answers before the server is really up (during its init it restarts); retrying `createdb` until it succeeds is the reliable wait.
- If `docker ps` fails, Docker Desktop is not running: say so and do the steps that need no database first.
- The `TEST_DATABASE_URL=… python …` prefix sets the variable for that one command only. The database name must contain `test` (`tests/conftest.py` refuses any other).
- Run one pytest at a time against it: two concurrent runs corrupt each other.
- Always `docker stop isyuku-pg` at the end, even after a failure. `--rm` then deletes it.

## The shared contract

**The two keys** (Task 1; mirrored by Task 6):

| Where | Text |
|---|---|
| `auth/permissions.py` `PERMISSIONS["workload.view"]`, appended after `quality.view` (the 30th key) | `"Open the İş yükü page and read each unit's workload for the current shift — patients, nurses, unassigned patients, late and remaining tasks, alarms waiting — as counts, and the shift's nurse–bed assignment (GET /shift-schedule)"` |
| `auth/permissions.py` `PERMISSIONS["shifts.manage"]` (§5 M5; was «Create shift schedules») | `"Assign nurses to beds for this shift or the next, unit by unit (the İş yükü page's assignment form; GET /shift-schedule/nurses, PUT /shift-schedule)"` |
| `catalogue.js` `PERMISSION_KEYS` | `"workload.view"`, last |
| `admin.testutil.js` `PERMISSION_TEXTS` | both texts, word for word |
| `perms.js` | `workload.view`: tr «“İş yükü” sayfasını açar: …»; `shifts.manage`: tr «Nöbet atamasını yapar: bu ve sonraki nöbet için, birim birim, hemşireleri yataklara atar» (Task 6 Step 6 has all eight) |
| Default groups | `charge_nurse` («Sorumlu Hemşire»): `DEFAULT_GROUPS` for a tenant made later, migration `0022` for the ones seeded before; `hospital_admin` by rule |

**The window** (`shift`, in every answer):

| Field | Type | Example | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `start` | string (ISO, UTC, `Z`) | `"2026-11-12T08:00:00Z"` | the window's first instant — its **key** (N1) |
| `end` | string (ISO, UTC) | `"2026-11-12T20:00:00Z"` | the next window's first instant |
| `which` | `"A"` \| `"B"` | `"A"` | A from `shift_start_a`, B from `shift_start_b` |

**`GET /shift-schedule?shift=current|next[&unit_id=<uuid>]`** (Task 3) — guard `shifts.manage` **or** `workload.view`; `shift` defaults to `current`:

| Field | Type | Example | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `shift` | the window | see above | «current» holds now, «next» follows it (N2) |
| `units` | array | `[{…}]` | the units in the caller's reach (N5), by name then id; one with `unit_id` |
| `units[].unit_id` | string (UUID) | `"33333333-3333-4333-8333-333333333301"` | the unit |
| `units[].unit_name` | string | `"Reanimasiya"` | its name |
| `units[].items` | array (`[]` when none) | `[{…}]` | the window's ACTIVE rows on the unit's beds (N4), by bed id |
| `units[].other_rows` | integer ≥ 0 | `0` | active rows of the unit of ANOTHER window that overlap this one (§5 A3) |
| `items[].bed_id` | string (UUID) | `"55555555-5555-4555-8555-555555555501"` | the bed |
| `items[].nurse_id` | string (UUID) | `"66666666-6666-4666-8666-666666666602"` | the nurse |
| `items[].nurse_name` | string | `"Leyla Sentetik"` | her full name, else her user name |
| `items[].nurse_username` | string | `"leyla@sentetik.example"` | her user name (two of one name, §5 A11) |
| `generated_at` | string (ISO) | `"2026-11-12T10:00:00Z"` | the server's time |

Errors: 403 `permission required: shifts.manage or workload.view`; with `unit_id`: 404 `{"detail": "Unit not found."}`, 403 `{"detail": {"code": "out_of_scope", …}}`; 422 for a `shift` other than the two. No patient anywhere (Task 3 pins the keys).

**`GET /shift-schedule/nurses?unit_id=<uuid>`** (Task 3) — guard `shifts.manage`; `unit_id` required and checked as above:

```json
[{"id": "66666666-6666-4666-8666-666666666601", "name": "Aysel Sentetik", "username": "aysel@sentetik.example", "reaches_unit": true},
 {"id": "66666666-6666-4666-8666-666666666604", "name": "Nigar Sentetik", "username": "nigar@sentetik.example", "reaches_unit": false}]
```

The tenant's active members of the default `nurse` or `charge_nurse` group, by name; `reaches_unit`: her own scope reaches the unit asked for (N8).

**`PUT /shift-schedule`** (Task 3) — guard `shifts.manage`. Request:

```json
{"unit_id": "33333333-3333-4333-8333-333333333301", "shift_start": "2026-11-12T08:00:00Z",
 "items": [{"bed_id": "55555555-5555-4555-8555-555555555501", "nurse_id": "66666666-6666-4666-8666-666666666602"}],
 "expected": [{"bed_id": "55555555-5555-4555-8555-555555555501", "nurse_id": "66666666-6666-4666-8666-666666666601"}]}
```

`shift_start`: the GET answer's `shift.start` (the window's key). `expected`: required, every row the form read for that unit and window (a set; `[]` means none). Answer 200: the `GET` answer for that one unit, plus

| Field | Type | Example | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `warnings` | array of `{code: "unit_not_reached", nurse_id}` | `[{"code": "unit_not_reached", "nurse_id": "6666…604"}]` | saved, but her board will not show these beds (N9) |

Errors (N6): 403 permission; 404 `Unit not found.` / `Bed not found.`; 403 `out_of_scope`; 422 `field_invalid:items` / `Kullanıcı bu kurumun üyesi değil` / a missing `expected`; **409 `{"detail": {"code": "shift_changed", …}}`** (the window is no longer current or next); **409 `{"detail": "Kayıt aynı anda değiştirildi; lütfen tekrar deneyin"}`** (`expected` no longer holds); **409 `{"detail": {"code": "assignment_conflict", …}}`** (the index); a POST to the path is 405.

**The audit row `shift.assign`** (Task 3; labelled in Task 11): `patient_id` `""`, `subject` the unit's id, payload

```json
{"unit_id": "33333333-3333-4333-8333-333333333301", "shift_start": "2026-11-12T08:00:00+00:00",
 "shift_end": "2026-11-12T20:00:00+00:00",
 "before": [["55555555-5555-4555-8555-555555555501", "66666666-6666-4666-8666-666666666601"]],
 "after": [["55555555-5555-4555-8555-555555555502", "66666666-6666-4666-8666-666666666602"]],
 "other": [], "actor": "charge@kabul.example.com"}
```

`dashboard/src/live/screens/isyuku/isyuku.testutil.js` (Task 9) holds the dashboard's synthetic answers in these shapes.

## Task 2 (engine): the shift windows — one wall-clock helper

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `homunculus-engine/tests/test_shiftclock.py` with:

```python
# homunculus-engine/tests/test_shiftclock.py
"""G4-04 (spec D3, §5 A2): the shift windows are bounded by the tenant's local wall-clock starts — A from `start_a`, B
from `start_b` to the next local `start_a` — found by a search over the candidate starts; a boundary instant begins the
new window; a window that crosses midnight belongs to the day it began; «Bu nöbet» and «Sonraki nöbet»; two starts not
twelve hours apart; the two nights a clock changes (13 and 11 hours). A window's key is the UTC instant of its local
start. Synthetic zones: tzdata is not on every test host (test_fluids.py), so the summer-time zone is written here."""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone, tzinfo

from homunculus.api.fluids import fluid_day
from homunculus.api.shiftclock import Shift, shift_windows

UTC = timezone.utc
BAKU = timezone(timedelta(hours=4))     # Asia/Baku keeps +04 all year
HOUR = timedelta(hours=1)


def _z(*args) -> datetime:
    return datetime(*args, tzinfo=UTC)


class _Summer(tzinfo):
    """A zone with a summer hour: +01:00, and +02:00 from SPRING to AUTUMN (UTC instants, both at 01:00 UTC as in
    Europe in 2026 — the clock jumps 02:00 → 03:00 on 29 March and falls 03:00 → 02:00 on 25 October). A wall time in
    the hour the clock repeats is told apart by `fold` (PEP 495)."""
    STD, SUMMER = timedelta(hours=1), timedelta(hours=2)
    SPRING, AUTUMN = datetime(2026, 3, 29, 1), datetime(2026, 10, 25, 1)        # naive UTC

    def utcoffset(self, dt):
        wall = dt.replace(tzinfo=None, fold=0)
        if self.SPRING + self.SUMMER <= wall < self.AUTUMN + self.STD:          # summer, past the skipped hour
            return self.SUMMER
        if self.AUTUMN + self.STD <= wall < self.AUTUMN + self.SUMMER:          # the repeated hour
            return self.STD if dt.fold else self.SUMMER
        return self.STD

    def dst(self, dt):
        return self.utcoffset(dt) - self.STD

    def tzname(self, dt):
        return "SUMMER" if self.dst(dt) else "STD"

    def fromutc(self, dt):
        utc = dt.replace(tzinfo=None)
        if self.SPRING <= utc < self.AUTUMN:
            return (utc + self.SUMMER).replace(tzinfo=self)
        repeated = self.AUTUMN <= utc < self.AUTUMN + HOUR
        return (utc + self.STD).replace(tzinfo=self, fold=1 if repeated else 0)


def current(now, zone=UTC, a=8, b=20) -> Shift:
    return shift_windows(now, zone, a, b)[0]


def following(now, zone=UTC, a=8, b=20) -> Shift:
    return shift_windows(now, zone, a, b)[1]


class TestBuNobet:
    def test_a_runs_from_the_first_start_to_the_second(self):
        assert current(_z(2026, 11, 12, 10)) == Shift(_z(2026, 11, 12, 8), _z(2026, 11, 12, 20), "A")

    def test_b_crosses_midnight_and_belongs_to_the_day_it_began(self):
        night = Shift(_z(2026, 11, 12, 20), _z(2026, 11, 13, 8), "B")
        assert current(_z(2026, 11, 12, 23)) == night
        assert current(_z(2026, 11, 13, 3)) == night

    def test_a_boundary_instant_begins_the_new_shift(self):
        assert current(_z(2026, 11, 12, 20)).which == "B"
        assert current(_z(2026, 11, 13, 7, 59, 59)).which == "B"
        assert current(_z(2026, 11, 13, 8)) == Shift(_z(2026, 11, 13, 8), _z(2026, 11, 13, 20), "A")

    def test_the_hours_are_the_tenants_own(self):
        # 05:00 UTC is 09:00 in Baku: shift A, 08:00–20:00 Baku = 04:00–16:00 UTC.
        assert current(_z(2026, 11, 12, 5), BAKU) == Shift(_z(2026, 11, 12, 4), _z(2026, 11, 12, 16), "A")
        # 03:00 UTC is 07:00 in Baku: still last night's B.
        assert current(_z(2026, 11, 12, 3), BAKU) == Shift(_z(2026, 11, 11, 16), _z(2026, 11, 12, 4), "B")

    def test_a_crosses_midnight_when_the_first_start_is_in_the_evening(self):
        assert current(_z(2026, 11, 13, 2), a=19, b=7) == Shift(_z(2026, 11, 12, 19), _z(2026, 11, 13, 7), "A")
        assert current(_z(2026, 11, 13, 9), a=19, b=7) == Shift(_z(2026, 11, 13, 7), _z(2026, 11, 13, 19), "B")

    def test_the_two_starts_need_not_be_twelve_hours_apart(self):
        assert current(_z(2026, 11, 12, 10), a=8, b=16) == Shift(_z(2026, 11, 12, 8), _z(2026, 11, 12, 16), "A")
        assert current(_z(2026, 11, 12, 17), a=8, b=16) == Shift(_z(2026, 11, 12, 16), _z(2026, 11, 13, 8), "B")


class TestSonrakiNobet:
    def test_after_a_comes_its_b_and_after_b_the_next_days_a(self):
        assert following(_z(2026, 11, 12, 10)) == Shift(_z(2026, 11, 12, 20), _z(2026, 11, 13, 8), "B")
        assert following(_z(2026, 11, 12, 23)) == Shift(_z(2026, 11, 13, 8), _z(2026, 11, 13, 20), "A")

    def test_in_the_tenants_zone(self):
        assert following(_z(2026, 11, 12, 20), BAKU) == Shift(_z(2026, 11, 13, 4), _z(2026, 11, 13, 16), "A")


class TestTheNightsTheClockChanges:
    """§5 A2: a window is bounded by the local wall clock, so the shifts tile time — no hour in no window, no two
    windows for one hour. `fluid_day` (G3-03) adds 24 hours to a UTC instant and is not changed in this slice; the
    two disagree on these nights only (plan note N1)."""
    ZONE = _Summer()

    def test_the_night_the_clock_goes_back_b_lasts_thirteen_hours(self):
        b = current(_z(2026, 10, 25, 6, 30), self.ZONE)                # 07:30 local, standard time again
        assert b == Shift(_z(2026, 10, 24, 18), _z(2026, 10, 25, 7), "B")
        assert b.end - b.start == 13 * HOUR
        assert current(_z(2026, 10, 25, 7), self.ZONE) == Shift(_z(2026, 10, 25, 7), _z(2026, 10, 25, 19), "A")
        assert following(_z(2026, 10, 24, 12), self.ZONE) == b         # «Sonraki nöbet» from the day before
        # The fluid day, unchanged, ends at 06:00 UTC — an hour before this window does (the recorded discrepancy).
        assert fluid_day(_z(2026, 10, 25, 6, 30), self.ZONE, 8, 20)["day"][1] == _z(2026, 10, 25, 6)

    def test_the_night_the_clock_goes_forward_b_lasts_eleven_hours(self):
        b = current(_z(2026, 3, 28, 22), self.ZONE)                    # 23:00 local, standard time
        assert b == Shift(_z(2026, 3, 28, 19), _z(2026, 3, 29, 6), "B")
        assert b.end - b.start == 11 * HOUR
        assert current(_z(2026, 3, 29, 6, 30), self.ZONE) == Shift(_z(2026, 3, 29, 6), _z(2026, 3, 29, 18), "A")
        assert following(_z(2026, 3, 28, 22), self.ZONE).start == b.end
```

Expected: a collection error, `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'homunculus.api.shiftclock'` (`1 error`).

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the helper**

Create `homunculus-engine/src/homunculus/api/shiftclock.py` with:

```python
# homunculus-engine/src/homunculus/api/shiftclock.py
"""The shift windows of «İş yükü» (G4-04, spec D3 and §5 A2): the shift that holds an instant, and the one after it.

The two starts are Görevlerim's `shift_start_a` / `shift_start_b` (api/tasks.py `TaskSettings`: whole hours in the
tenant's zone, never equal, not necessarily twelve hours apart). A window is bounded by LOCAL wall-clock boundaries: A
runs from a day's `start_a` o'clock to its `start_b` o'clock, B from there to the next local `start_a` — so a window
is 11 or 13 hours the nights a clock changes, and not 12 at all when the two starts are not twelve hours apart. The
boundaries are found by a search over the candidates of the days around the instant (as `api/tasks.py` `shift_window`
finds Görevlerim's list window); a boundary instant begins the new window. A window's key is the UTC instant of its
local start (`nurse_bed_assignments.shift_start`).

`api/fluids.py` `fluid_day` adds 12 and 24 hours to a UTC instant instead, and is not changed in this slice (Asia/Baku
keeps no summer time): the two disagree only on the night a clock changes (plan note N1)."""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Shift:
    """One shift: its UTC bounds, and which of the day's two it is — "A" from `start_a`, "B" from `start_b`."""
    start: datetime
    end: datetime
    which: str


def shift_windows(now: datetime, zone, start_a: int, start_b: int) -> tuple[Shift, Shift]:
    """(«Bu nöbet», «Sonraki nöbet»): the window that holds `now`, and the one after it."""
    day = now.astimezone(zone).date()
    starts = sorted(
        (datetime(d.year, d.month, d.day, hour, 0, tzinfo=zone).astimezone(timezone.utc), which)
        for d in (day + timedelta(days=k) for k in (-1, 0, 1, 2))
        for hour, which in ((start_a, "A"), (start_b, "B")))
    at = max(i for i, (start, _) in enumerate(starts) if start <= now)
    (first, which), (second, then), (third, _) = starts[at], starts[at + 1], starts[at + 2]
    return Shift(first, second, which), Shift(second, third, then)
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing DB-backed tests**

Create `homunculus-engine/tests/test_shift_schedule_api.py` with:

```python
# homunculus-engine/tests/test_shift_schedule_api.py
"""G4-04 «İş yükü» (spec D3, D4 and §5): the nurse–bed assignment per shift.
- GET /shift-schedule answers the window and the units in the caller's reach — her unit grants (a bed grant reaches no
  unit), every unit for an unrestricted caller — each with its ACTIVE rows of that window (a row's unit is its bed's)
  and the count of its active rows of another window that overlap this one (`other_rows`: the shift hours changed);
  either key opens it; staff names, never a patient;
- GET /shift-schedule/nurses offers the tenant's active members of the default nurse and charge-nurse groups, each with
  whether her own scope reaches the unit;
- PUT /shift-schedule names its window by `shift_start` (a window no longer current or next is a 409 `shift_changed`)
  and replaces one unit's window under its lock: the rows it ends go inactive with `closed_at` and stay, the new ones are
  written, the rows of another window that overlap it end too; `expected` is required and must be what holds (409);
  a bed of another unit, a closed bed, a person not offered or a bed named twice is a 422 — a person no longer offered
  keeps the bed she holds; a unit outside her units is a 403; two saves on one read at once save one; a nurse who
  cannot see the unit is a warning; one `shift.assign` audit row, ids only, the unit its subject;
- the window is the tenant's own (its two starts, its zone); the POST is gone.
Postgres. Synthetic ward: tests/kabul_world.py."""
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import pytest
import pytest_asyncio
from sqlalchemy import select, text, update

from homunculus.admin.service import CONCURRENT_CHANGE
from homunculus.admission import shifts
from homunculus.admission.models import NurseBedAssignment
from homunculus.api.tasks import InMemoryTaskStore, TaskSettings
from homunculus.auth.groups import ensure_default_groups
from homunculus.auth.models import Group, GroupPermission, TenantMembership, User
from homunculus.scope.models import StaffScope
from homunculus.tenant.models import Bed, Tenant
from tests.conftest import requires_db
from tests.kabul_world import SLUG, build_ward, ward_client
from tests.signed_in import add_user, bearer
from tests.test_discharge_api import audited

pytestmark = [requires_db]
#: 10:00 UTC on 12 November: shift A of the default starts (08 and 20) in the ward's zone, UTC.
NOW = datetime(2026, 11, 12, 10, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
A_START, A_END = datetime(2026, 11, 12, 8, tzinfo=timezone.utc), datetime(2026, 11, 12, 20, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
CURRENT = {"start": "2026-11-12T08:00:00Z", "end": "2026-11-12T20:00:00Z", "which": "A"}
NEXT = {"start": "2026-11-12T20:00:00Z", "end": "2026-11-13T08:00:00Z", "which": "B"}


@pytest_asyncio.fixture
async def ward(db_session, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.delenv("SCOPE_ENFORCE_UNASSIGNED", raising=False)
    monkeypatch.setattr(shifts, "_now", lambda: NOW)
    built = await build_ward(db_session)
    async with ward_client(db_session) as (client, app):
        yield built, client, app


def _pairs(w, pairs):
    return [{"bed_id": str(w.beds[bed].id), "nurse_id": str(w.users[who].id)} for bed, who in pairs]


def _put(w, unit="rea", items=(), start=CURRENT["start"], expected=()) -> dict:
    """A PUT body: (bed label, user) pairs for the window starting at `start`; `expected` the pairs the form read."""
    return {"unit_id": str(w.units[unit].id), "shift_start": start, "items": _pairs(w, items),
            "expected": _pairs(w, expected)}


def _shown(w, answer) -> dict[str, list[tuple[str, str]]]:
    """The answer's units by name, each with its (bed label, nurse name) pairs."""
    label = {str(bed.id): name for name, bed in w.beds.items()}
    return {u["unit_name"]: sorted((label[i["bed_id"]], i["nurse_name"]) for i in u["items"]) for u in answer["units"]}


async def _rows(db, w) -> list[tuple[str, str, bool, datetime]]:
    """Every row of the table as (bed label, user, active, window start), sorted."""
    beds = {bed.id: name for name, bed in w.beds.items()}
    users = {user.id: name for name, user in w.users.items()}
    rows = (await db.execute(select(NurseBedAssignment.bed_id, NurseBedAssignment.nurse_id,
                                    NurseBedAssignment.is_active, NurseBedAssignment.shift_start))).all()
    return sorted((beds[bed], users.get(nurse, "?"), active, start) for bed, nurse, active, start in rows)


async def _holder(db, w, name: str, *keys: str, unit: str | None = None, bed: str | None = None) -> dict[str, str]:
    """A member of a group of the ward's own that holds `keys` alone, granted `unit` or `bed`; her bearer header."""
    group = Group(tenant_id=w.tenant.id, slug=f"g_{name}", name=name, is_system=False)
    group.permissions = [GroupPermission(permission=key) for key in keys]
    db.add(group)
    await db.flush()
    user = await add_user(db, username=f"{name}@kabul.example.com", tenant_id=w.tenant.id, group=group)
    if unit or bed:
        db.add(StaffScope(tenant_id=w.tenant.id, user_id=user.id, unit_id=str(w.units[unit].id) if unit else "",
                          bed_id=str(w.beds[bed].id) if bed else ""))
        await db.commit()
    return await bearer(db, user)


async def _nurse(db, w, username: str):
    """One more member of the ward's default nurse group (no grant)."""
    group = (await db.execute(select(Group).where(Group.tenant_id == w.tenant.id, Group.slug == "nurse"))).scalar_one()
    return await add_user(db, username=username, tenant_id=w.tenant.id, group=group)


async def _waits_on_the_unit(db_session) -> None:
    """Until a session of the test database waits on a lock while it reads the units table (the PUT's lock). A fresh
    connection per look: a transaction would see `pg_stat_activity` as it was when it first read it."""
    for _ in range(200):
        async with db_session.bind.connect() as conn:
            waiting = (await conn.execute(text(
                "SELECT count(*) FROM pg_stat_activity WHERE datname = current_database() "
                "AND wait_event_type = 'Lock' AND query NOT ILIKE '%advisory%' AND query ILIKE '%units%'"))).scalar_one()
        if waiting:
            return
        await asyncio.sleep(0.05)
    raise AssertionError("the second save never waited on the unit's lock")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- GET /shift-schedule
async def test_the_shift_and_the_units_in_reach_with_their_active_rows(ward, db_session):
    """D3, D4: «Bu nöbet» holds now (10:00 UTC: A, 08–20); an unrestricted caller reads every unit, by name, a quiet one
    with no row; only the ACTIVE rows of THIS window — an ended row and the next window's are not."""
    w, client, _ = ward
    rea, b1, b2 = w.units["rea"].id, w.beds["1"].id, w.beds["2"].id
    nurse, charge = w.users["nurse"].id, w.users["charge"].id
    db_session.add_all([
        NurseBedAssignment(unit_id=rea, bed_id=b1, nurse_id=nurse, shift_start=A_START, shift_end=A_END),
        NurseBedAssignment(unit_id=rea, bed_id=b2, nurse_id=charge, shift_start=A_START, shift_end=A_END,
                           is_active=False),
        NurseBedAssignment(unit_id=rea, bed_id=b2, nurse_id=nurse, shift_start=A_END,
                           shift_end=A_END + timedelta(hours=12)),
    ])
    await db_session.commit()
    r = await client.get("/shift-schedule", headers=w.auth["admin"])
    assert r.status_code == 200, r.text
    assert r.json()["shift"] == CURRENT
    assert [(u["unit_name"], u["other_rows"]) for u in r.json()["units"]] == [("Neonatal", 0), ("Reanimasiya", 0)]
    assert _shown(w, r.json()) == {"Neonatal": [], "Reanimasiya": [("1", "nurse")]}
    r = await client.get("/shift-schedule?shift=next", headers=w.auth["admin"])
    assert (r.json()["shift"], _shown(w, r.json())) == (NEXT, {"Neonatal": [], "Reanimasiya": [("2", "nurse")]})


async def test_a_rows_unit_is_its_beds_and_the_answer_names_no_patient(ward, db_session):
    """§5 A8: a row the retired POST wrote under another unit is its bed's unit's. The keys are pinned, so a patient
    field cannot slip in; staff by full name and user name."""
    w, client, _ = ward
    db_session.add(NurseBedAssignment(unit_id=w.units["neo"].id, bed_id=w.beds["1"].id, nurse_id=w.users["nurse"].id,
                                      shift_start=A_START, shift_end=A_END))
    await db_session.commit()
    body = (await client.get("/shift-schedule", headers=w.auth["admin"])).json()
    assert _shown(w, body) == {"Neonatal": [], "Reanimasiya": [("1", "nurse")]}
    assert set(body) == {"shift", "units", "generated_at"} and set(body["shift"]) == {"start", "end", "which"}
    assert {key for unit in body["units"] for key in unit} == {"unit_id", "unit_name", "items", "other_rows"}
    (item,) = [i for unit in body["units"] for i in unit["items"]]
    assert item == {"bed_id": str(w.beds["1"].id), "nurse_id": str(w.users["nurse"].id), "nurse_name": "nurse",
                    "nurse_username": "nurse@kabul.example.com"}
    assert body["generated_at"] == "2026-11-12T10:00:00Z"


async def test_the_reach_is_the_callers_unit_grants(ward, db_session):
    """D4, §5 M3: a unit grant reaches its unit (the charge nurse: Reanimasiya); a bed grant reaches no unit; a unit asked
    for outside her units is a 403 (another tenant's or a missing one a 404: tests/test_tenant_isolation.py)."""
    w, client, _ = ward
    r = await client.get("/shift-schedule", headers=w.auth["charge"])
    assert [u["unit_name"] for u in r.json()["units"]] == ["Reanimasiya"]
    bed_only = await _holder(db_session, w, "bedonly", "workload.view", bed="1")
    r = await client.get("/shift-schedule", headers=bed_only)
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["units"]) == (200, [])
    r = await client.get(f"/shift-schedule?unit_id={w.units['neo'].id}", headers=w.auth["charge"])
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["detail"]["code"]) == (403, "out_of_scope")
    r = await client.get(f"/shift-schedule?shift=next&unit_id={w.units['rea'].id}", headers=w.auth["charge"])
    assert ([u["unit_name"] for u in r.json()["units"]], r.json()["shift"]) == (["Reanimasiya"], NEXT)


async def test_either_key_opens_the_read_and_the_writes_are_the_managers(ward, db_session):
    """D4: GET for `shifts.manage` or `workload.view`; the form's people and the PUT for `shifts.manage` alone."""
    w, client, _ = ward
    viewer = await _holder(db_session, w, "viewer", "workload.view", unit="rea")
    manager = await _holder(db_session, w, "manager", "shifts.manage", unit="rea")
    for headers in (viewer, manager):
        assert (await client.get("/shift-schedule", headers=headers)).status_code == 200
    rea = w.units["rea"].id
    r = await client.get(f"/shift-schedule/nurses?unit_id={rea}", headers=viewer)
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["detail"]) == (403, "permission required: shifts.manage")
    assert (await client.put("/shift-schedule", json=_put(w), headers=viewer)).status_code == 403
    assert (await client.get(f"/shift-schedule/nurses?unit_id={rea}", headers=manager)).status_code == 200
    r = await client.get("/shift-schedule", headers=w.auth["auditor"])
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["detail"]) == (403, "permission required: shifts.manage or workload.view")


async def test_the_window_is_the_tenants_own_starts_and_zone(ward, monkeypatch):
    """D3, §5 M4: Görevlerim's two starts and the tenant's zone, read from the app's task store as GET /tasks reads them."""
    w, client, app = ward
    app.state.tasks.save_settings(SLUG, TaskSettings(shift_start_a=7, shift_start_b=19), by="admin", now=NOW)
    r = await client.get("/shift-schedule", headers=w.auth["charge"])
    assert r.json()["shift"] == {"start": "2026-11-12T07:00:00Z", "end": "2026-11-12T19:00:00Z", "which": "A"}
    baku = timezone(timedelta(hours=4))       # tzdata is not on every test host (test_fluids.py)
    app.state.tasks = InMemoryTaskStore(zones={SLUG: "Asia/Baku"})
    monkeypatch.setattr(shifts, "zone_of", lambda name: baku if name == "Asia/Baku" else timezone.utc)
    r = await client.get("/shift-schedule", headers=w.auth["charge"])
    # 10:00 UTC is 14:00 in Baku: A, 08:00–20:00 Baku.
    assert r.json()["shift"] == {"start": "2026-11-12T04:00:00Z", "end": "2026-11-12T16:00:00Z", "which": "A"}


async def test_rows_of_shift_hours_changed_mid_shift_are_counted_and_the_next_save_ends_them(ward, db_session):
    """§5 A3: the hospital moves its hours to 07/19 at 10:00; the 08–20 rows no longer have the window's key — the GET
    counts them (`other_rows`), and a PUT for the new window ends them too, said in its audit row."""
    w, client, app = ward
    db_session.add(NurseBedAssignment(unit_id=w.units["rea"].id, bed_id=w.beds["1"].id, nurse_id=w.users["nurse"].id,
                                      shift_start=A_START, shift_end=A_END))
    await db_session.commit()
    app.state.tasks.save_settings(SLUG, TaskSettings(shift_start_a=7, shift_start_b=19), by="admin", now=NOW)
    r = await client.get("/shift-schedule", headers=w.auth["charge"])
    (unit,) = r.json()["units"]
    assert (unit["items"], unit["other_rows"]) == ([], 1)
    r = await client.put("/shift-schedule", json=_put(w, items=[("2", "nurse")], start="2026-11-12T07:00:00Z"),
                         headers=w.auth["charge"])
    assert r.status_code == 200, r.text
    assert r.json()["units"][0]["other_rows"] == 0
    seven = datetime(2026, 11, 12, 7, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    assert await _rows(db_session, w) == [("1", "nurse", False, A_START), ("2", "nurse", True, seven)]
    (row,) = audited(app, "shift.assign")
    assert row.payload["other"] == [[str(w.beds["1"].id), str(w.users["nurse"].id)]]


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- GET /shift-schedule/nurses
async def test_the_form_offers_the_tenants_active_nurses_and_charge_nurses_and_says_who_reaches_the_unit(ward, db_session):
    """§5 A10: the members of the default `nurse` and `charge_nurse` groups — not the physician, the administrator or the
    auditor; not a deactivated account, a membership no longer active, or another tenant's nurse — by name, each with
    whether her own scope reaches the unit (the second nurse is granted Neonatal only). The unit is checked as the PUT
    checks it."""
    w, client, _ = ward
    gone = await _nurse(db_session, w, "gone@kabul.example.com")
    left = await _nurse(db_session, w, "left@kabul.example.com")
    await db_session.execute(update(User).where(User.id == gone.id).values(is_active=False))
    await db_session.execute(update(TenantMembership).where(TenantMembership.user_id == left.id).values(is_active=False))
    other = Tenant(name="Sentetik Başka Hastane", slug="baska-hastane", country_code="AZ")
    db_session.add(other)
    await db_session.flush()
    groups = await ensure_default_groups(db_session, other.id)
    await db_session.commit()
    await add_user(db_session, username="uzak@baska.example.com", tenant_id=other.id, group=groups["nurse"])
    r = await client.get(f"/shift-schedule/nurses?unit_id={w.units['rea'].id}", headers=w.auth["charge"])
    assert r.status_code == 200, r.text
    assert r.json() == [
        {"id": str(w.users[name].id), "name": name, "username": f"{name}@kabul.example.com", "reaches_unit": reaches}
        for name, reaches in (("charge", True), ("nurse", True), ("nurse_b", False))]
    r = await client.get(f"/shift-schedule/nurses?unit_id={w.units['neo'].id}", headers=w.auth["charge"])
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["detail"]["code"]) == (403, "out_of_scope")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- PUT /shift-schedule
async def test_a_save_replaces_the_units_window_and_keeps_the_rows_it_ended(ward, db_session):
    """D4, §5 M1: a row the save leaves out goes inactive with `closed_at` and stays; a pair it keeps is not touched; a
    new pair is written. The answer is the unit's new state."""
    w, client, _ = ward
    r = await client.put("/shift-schedule", json=_put(w, items=[("1", "nurse"), ("2", "nurse")]), headers=w.auth["charge"])
    assert r.status_code == 200, r.text
    assert (r.json()["shift"], _shown(w, r.json()), r.json()["warnings"]) == (
        CURRENT, {"Reanimasiya": [("1", "nurse"), ("2", "nurse")]}, [])
    kept = (await db_session.execute(select(NurseBedAssignment.id).where(
        NurseBedAssignment.bed_id == w.beds["2"].id))).scalar_one()
    r = await client.put("/shift-schedule", json=_put(w, items=[("1", "charge"), ("2", "nurse")],
                                                      expected=[("1", "nurse"), ("2", "nurse")]),
                         headers=w.auth["charge"])
    assert r.status_code == 200, r.text
    assert _shown(w, r.json()) == {"Reanimasiya": [("1", "charge"), ("2", "nurse")]}
    assert await _rows(db_session, w) == [("1", "charge", True, A_START), ("1", "nurse", False, A_START),
                                          ("2", "nurse", True, A_START)]
    closed = (await db_session.execute(select(NurseBedAssignment.closed_at).where(
        NurseBedAssignment.is_active.is_(False)))).scalar_one()
    assert closed == NOW
    still = (await db_session.execute(select(NurseBedAssignment.id).where(
        NurseBedAssignment.bed_id == w.beds["2"].id, NurseBedAssignment.is_active.is_(True)))).scalar_one()
    assert still == kept                                       # the kept pair's own row


async def test_the_next_shift_is_a_window_of_its_own(ward):
    w, client, _ = ward
    r = await client.put("/shift-schedule", json=_put(w, items=[("1", "nurse")], start=NEXT["start"]),
                         headers=w.auth["charge"])
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["shift"]) == (200, NEXT)
    assert _shown(w, (await client.get("/shift-schedule", headers=w.auth["charge"])).json()) == {"Reanimasiya": []}
    r = await client.get("/shift-schedule?shift=next", headers=w.auth["charge"])
    assert _shown(w, r.json()) == {"Reanimasiya": [("1", "nurse")]}


async def test_a_save_across_the_shift_change_names_the_window_it_meant(ward, monkeypatch):
    """§5 A1: the form read at 19:50 and saves at 20:00:30. Its «Bu nöbet» (08:00) is neither current nor next any more:
    409 `shift_changed`, nothing written. Its «Sonraki nöbet» (20:00) is current now: saved there."""
    w, client, app = ward
    monkeypatch.setattr(shifts, "_now", lambda: datetime(2026, 11, 12, 20, 0, 30, tzinfo=timezone.utc))
    r = await client.put("/shift-schedule", json=_put(w, items=[("1", "nurse")]), headers=w.auth["charge"])
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["detail"]["code"]) == (409, "shift_changed")
    assert audited(app, "shift.assign") == []
    r = await client.put("/shift-schedule", json=_put(w, items=[("1", "nurse")], start=NEXT["start"]),
                         headers=w.auth["charge"])
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["shift"]) == (200, NEXT)
    r = await client.put("/shift-schedule", json=_put(w, start="2026-11-12T07:00:00Z"), headers=w.auth["charge"])
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["detail"]["code"]) == (409, "shift_changed")     # never a window's start at all


async def test_an_empty_list_clears_the_window_and_expected_is_required(ward, db_session):
    """§5 A7: `expected` is required — a body without it is refused; an empty `items` ends every row of the window."""
    w, client, _ = ward
    await client.put("/shift-schedule", json=_put(w, items=[("1", "nurse")]), headers=w.auth["charge"])
    body = _put(w, items=[])
    del body["expected"]
    assert (await client.put("/shift-schedule", json=body, headers=w.auth["charge"])).status_code == 422
    r = await client.put("/shift-schedule", json=_put(w, items=[], expected=[("1", "nurse")]), headers=w.auth["charge"])
    assert (r.status_code, _shown(w, r.json())) == (200, {"Reanimasiya": []})
    assert await _rows(db_session, w) == [("1", "nurse", False, A_START)]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("case", ["another unit's bed", "a closed bed", "a person not offered", "a bed named twice"])
async def test_a_wrong_item_is_a_422_and_nothing_is_written(ward, db_session, case):
    """D4: every new pair's bed an open bed of THAT unit, its person an offered one, a bed once — else 422
    `field_invalid:items`, nothing written, nothing audited."""
    w, client, app = ward
    if case == "a closed bed":
        await db_session.execute(update(Bed).where(Bed.id == w.beds["10"].id).values(is_active=False))
        await db_session.commit()
    items = {"another unit's bed": [("N1", "nurse")], "a closed bed": [("10", "nurse")],
             "a person not offered": [("1", "doctor")], "a bed named twice": [("1", "nurse"), ("1", "charge")]}[case]
    r = await client.put("/shift-schedule", json=_put(w, items=items), headers=w.auth["charge"])
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["detail"]["code"]) == (422, "field_invalid:items"), r.text
    assert await _rows(db_session, w) == [] and audited(app, "shift.assign") == []


async def test_a_nurse_no_longer_offered_keeps_her_bed_but_is_given_no_new_one(ward, db_session):
    """§5 A10: her account ends mid-shift; a save that keeps her pair as read is taken; one that gives her another bed is
    refused, nothing written."""
    w, client, _ = ward
    gone = await _nurse(db_session, w, "gone@kabul.example.com")
    w.users["gone"] = gone
    r = await client.put("/shift-schedule", json=_put(w, items=[("1", "gone")]), headers=w.auth["charge"])
    assert r.status_code == 200, r.text
    await db_session.execute(update(User).where(User.id == gone.id).values(is_active=False))
    await db_session.commit()
    r = await client.put("/shift-schedule", json=_put(w, items=[("1", "gone"), ("2", "nurse")], expected=[("1", "gone")]),
                         headers=w.auth["charge"])
    assert r.status_code == 200, r.text
    before = await _rows(db_session, w)
    r = await client.put("/shift-schedule", json=_put(w, items=[("1", "gone"), ("3", "gone")],
                                                      expected=[("1", "gone"), ("2", "nurse")]),
                         headers=w.auth["charge"])
    assert r.status_code == 422, r.text
    assert await _rows(db_session, w) == before


async def test_a_unit_outside_her_units_is_a_403_and_a_bed_grant_is_not_enough(ward, db_session):
    w, client, app = ward
    r = await client.put("/shift-schedule", json=_put(w, unit="neo", items=[("N1", "nurse_b")]),
                         headers=w.auth["charge"])
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["detail"]["code"]) == (403, "out_of_scope")
    bed_only = await _holder(db_session, w, "bedonly", "shifts.manage", bed="1")
    r = await client.put("/shift-schedule", json=_put(w, items=[("1", "nurse")]), headers=bed_only)
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["detail"]["code"]) == (403, "out_of_scope")
    assert await _rows(db_session, w) == [] and audited(app, "shift.assign") == []


async def test_a_save_on_an_old_read_is_a_409_and_writes_nothing(ward, db_session):
    """D4: `expected` is compared, as a set, with the rows read under the lock — the text the dashboard knows as
    `concurrentChange`, nothing written, nothing audited."""
    w, client, app = ward
    first = await client.put("/shift-schedule", json=_put(w, items=[("1", "nurse")]), headers=w.auth["charge"])
    assert first.status_code == 200, first.text
    before = await _rows(db_session, w)
    r = await client.put("/shift-schedule", json=_put(w, items=[("1", "nurse_b")]), headers=w.auth["admin"])
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["detail"]) == (409, CONCURRENT_CHANGE)
    assert await _rows(db_session, w) == before
    assert len(audited(app, "shift.assign")) == 1


async def test_two_saves_on_one_read_at_once_save_one_and_refuse_the_other(ward, db_session, monkeypatch):
    """§5 A7: the second save waits on the unit's lock, then reads the first's rows and is refused 409; one audit row."""
    w, client, app = ward
    reached, gate = asyncio.Event(), asyncio.Event()
    real, calls = shifts._held, []

    async def held(*args, **kwargs):
        rows = await real(*args, **kwargs)
        calls.append(1)
        if len(calls) == 1:
            reached.set()
            await gate.wait()
        return rows

    monkeypatch.setattr(shifts, "_held", held)
    one = asyncio.create_task(client.put("/shift-schedule", json=_put(w, items=[("1", "nurse")]),
                                         headers=w.auth["charge"]))
    try:
        await asyncio.wait_for(reached.wait(), timeout=10)
        two = asyncio.create_task(client.put("/shift-schedule", json=_put(w, items=[("1", "nurse_b")]),
                                             headers=w.auth["admin"]))
        await _waits_on_the_unit(db_session)
    finally:
        gate.set()
    first, second = await one, await two
    assert (first.status_code, second.status_code) == (200, 409), (first.text, second.text)
    assert await _rows(db_session, w) == [("1", "nurse", True, A_START)]
    assert len(audited(app, "shift.assign")) == 1


async def test_a_nurse_who_cannot_see_the_unit_is_a_warning_not_a_refusal(ward):
    """§5 A10, A12: the second nurse is granted Neonatal only — assigned a Reanimasiya bed she is saved, and the answer
    warns (ids: the page names her). No beds-per-nurse warning (S-25): four beds to one nurse warn nothing."""
    w, client, _ = ward
    r = await client.put("/shift-schedule", json=_put(w, items=[("1", "nurse_b"), ("2", "nurse"), ("3", "nurse"),
                                                                ("10", "nurse")]), headers=w.auth["charge"])
    assert r.status_code == 200, r.text
    assert r.json()["warnings"] == [{"code": "unit_not_reached", "nurse_id": str(w.users["nurse_b"].id)}]
    assert len(_shown(w, r.json())["Reanimasiya"]) == 4


async def test_one_audit_row_names_the_window_and_the_pairs_before_and_after(ward, db_session):
    """D4, §5 M8: `shift.assign` after the commit — the unit (its subject too), the window's bounds, the (bed, nurse) ids
    before and after, the other windows' rows it ended, the actor's user name; no patient, no name. The rows name the
    charge nurse who saved them."""
    w, client, app = ward
    await client.put("/shift-schedule", json=_put(w, items=[("1", "nurse")]), headers=w.auth["charge"])
    await client.put("/shift-schedule", json=_put(w, items=[("2", "charge")], expected=[("1", "nurse")]),
                     headers=w.auth["charge"])
    rows = audited(app, "shift.assign")
    assert len(rows) == 2
    ids = lambda bed, who: [str(w.beds[bed].id), str(w.users[who].id)]       # noqa: E731
    assert (rows[-1].patient_id, rows[-1].subject, rows[-1].payload) == ("", str(w.units["rea"].id), {
        "unit_id": str(w.units["rea"].id), "shift_start": "2026-11-12T08:00:00+00:00",
        "shift_end": "2026-11-12T20:00:00+00:00", "before": [ids("1", "nurse")], "after": [ids("2", "charge")],
        "other": [], "actor": "charge@kabul.example.com"})
    by = (await db_session.execute(select(NurseBedAssignment.charge_nurse_id)
                                   .where(NurseBedAssignment.is_active.is_(True)))).scalars().all()
    assert by == [w.users["charge"].id]


async def test_the_retired_post_is_gone(ward):
    """D4: POST /shift-schedule is retired — the path answers GET and PUT only."""
    w, client, _ = ward
    r = await client.post("/shift-schedule", json={}, headers=w.auth["charge"])
    assert r.status_code == 405
```

Replace with:

```python
    is_active: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=True, nullable=False)
```

```python
    __table_args__ = (

        Index(NURSE_BED_ACTIVE_INDEX, "bed_id", "shift_start", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=_NURSE_BED_ACTIVE, sqlite_where=_NURSE_BED_ACTIVE),
    )
```

```python
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Nurse–bed assignment per shift — §4.4; G4-04 «İş yükü» (spec D4, §5), admission/shifts.py
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
class ShiftWindow(BaseModel):
    """One of the tenant's two daily shifts (api/shiftclock.py): its UTC bounds, and which it is."""
    start: datetime
    end: datetime
    which: Literal["A", "B"]


class ShiftItem(BaseModel):
    """One bed and the nurse who looks after it in the window: staff by name (and user name, for two of one name),
    never a patient."""
    bed_id: uuid.UUID
    nurse_id: uuid.UUID
    nurse_name: str
    nurse_username: str


class ShiftUnit(BaseModel):
    unit_id: uuid.UUID
    unit_name: str
    items: list[ShiftItem]
    #: §5 A3: the unit's active rows of ANOTHER window that overlap this one — the shift hours changed meanwhile.
    other_rows: int


class ShiftSchedule(BaseModel):
    """GET /shift-schedule: the window, the units in the caller's reach with their active rows, the server's time."""
    shift: ShiftWindow
    units: list[ShiftUnit]
    generated_at: datetime


class ShiftWarning(BaseModel):
    """A soft warning of PUT /shift-schedule (§5 A10): `unit_not_reached` — this nurse's own scope does not reach the
    unit, so her board will not show these beds' patients. Saved all the same. Ids: the page names her."""
    code: Literal["unit_not_reached"]
    nurse_id: uuid.UUID


class ShiftScheduleSaved(ShiftSchedule):
    """PUT /shift-schedule: the unit's new state, and the soft warnings — never a refusal (no beds-per-nurse one, A12)."""
    warnings: list[ShiftWarning] = Field(default_factory=list)


class ShiftPair(BaseModel):
    """A bed and its nurse, as the PUT names them."""
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    bed_id: uuid.UUID
    nurse_id: uuid.UUID


class ShiftSchedulePut(BaseModel):
    """PUT /shift-schedule (D4, §5 A1, A7): replace one unit's assignment for the window starting at `shift_start` (the
    GET answer's `shift.start`). `expected`, required: the active rows the form read for that unit and window (a set;
    an empty list means none); an empty `items` clears the window."""
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    unit_id: uuid.UUID
    shift_start: AwareDatetime
    items: list[ShiftPair]
    expected: list[ShiftPair]


class ShiftNurse(BaseModel):
    """GET /shift-schedule/nurses: a person the form offers — her id, her name, her user name (two of one name are told
    apart by it, A11), and whether her own scope reaches the unit asked for (A10)."""
    id: uuid.UUID
    name: str
    username: str
    reaches_unit: bool
```

```python
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Nurse–bed shift assignment — §4.4, S-25
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
async def create_shift_schedule(
    db: AsyncSession, *, unit_id: uuid.UUID, charge_nurse_id: uuid.UUID,
    assignments: list[dict],
) -> tuple[list[NurseBedAssignment], list[str]]:
    """
    Create bed–nurse assignments for a shift.

    S-25: exceeding the unit's max_beds_per_nurse is a SOFT warning (not a hard
    block); the over-capacity condition is surfaced and audit-logged by the caller.
    """
    unit = await db.get(Unit, unit_id)
    if unit is None:
        raise WorkflowError("Unit not found.")

    warnings: list[str] = []
    beds_per_nurse: dict[uuid.UUID, int] = {}
    for a in assignments:
        beds_per_nurse[a["nurse_id"]] = beds_per_nurse.get(a["nurse_id"], 0) + 1

    for nurse_id, count in beds_per_nurse.items():
        if count > unit.max_beds_per_nurse:
            warnings.append(
                f"Nurse {nurse_id} is assigned {count} beds, exceeding the unit "
                f"maximum of {unit.max_beds_per_nurse} (soft limit, S-25)."
            )

    created: list[NurseBedAssignment] = []
    for a in assignments:
        row = NurseBedAssignment(
            unit_id=unit_id,
            bed_id=a["bed_id"],
            nurse_id=a["nurse_id"],
            charge_nurse_id=charge_nurse_id,
            shift_start=a["shift_start"],
            shift_end=a["shift_end"],
        )
        db.add(row)
        created.append(row)

    await db.flush()
    return created, warnings


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Transfer — §4.6, S-27
```

```python
async def test_shift_schedule_soft_overcapacity_warning(db_session):

    start, end = _now(), _now() + timedelta(hours=8)
```
