Create `homunculus-engine/src/homunculus/admission/shifts.py` with:

```python
# homunculus-engine/src/homunculus/admission/shifts.py
"""G4-04 «İş yükü» (spec docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-12-g4-is-yuku-design.md D3, D4 and §5): who looks after which
bed this shift — the charge nurse's assignment, and its read for the workload page.

  GET  /shift-schedule?shift=current|next[&unit_id=]   shifts.manage or workload.view
  GET  /shift-schedule/nurses?unit_id=                 shifts.manage
  PUT  /shift-schedule                                 shifts.manage

THE WINDOW. One of the tenant's two daily shifts (api/shiftclock.py `shift_windows`: local wall-clock starts), by
Görevlerim's two starts and the tenant's zone read as GET /tasks reads them: «current» holds now, «next» follows it. A
row belongs to the window whose start is its `shift_start` (the window's key). The PUT names its window by that key
(`shift_start`, from the GET the form read) and takes it only while it is still the current or the next window: a form
read at 19:50 and saved at 20:00:30 is a 409 `shift_changed`, never a write into the window after the one it showed
(§5 A1). A row of another window that overlaps the one read (the hours were changed meanwhile) is counted
(`other_rows`), and the next save of the window ends it (A3).

THE UNIT. A row is the unit of its BED (bed → room → unit), and is read so: the retired POST let a row name a bed of
another unit (A8). The caller's reach is her UNIT grants (`unitscope.unit_clause`): a bed grant names one bed, not the
unit around it, so it reaches no unit here; an unrestricted caller (the administrator) reaches every unit of her
tenant.

THE WRITE. One unit and one window at a time: in one transaction, under the unit's row lock — the lock every structure
write in the unit takes first (tenant/router.py `_lock_unit`, FOR NO KEY UPDATE) — the window's active rows are read
again and must equal `expected` (required; else 409 with the words the dashboard knows, `CONCURRENT_CHANGE`, as PUT
/scope/{user_id}); a row the save leaves out goes `is_active = false` with `closed_at` (kept: the history), a pair it
keeps stays as it is, a new pair is inserted after the ends are flushed (the partial unique index of migration 0022 —
one active nurse per bed per window — cannot be deferred). A unique violation is the coded 409 `assignment_conflict`,
never a 500. Audited `shift.assign` after the commit, ids only, the unit its subject.

WHO. The offered people are `service.nurse_ids` (the default nurse and charge-nurse groups, A10). A new pair names an
offered person; a person no longer offered (her account or membership ended mid-shift) keeps a pair she already holds.
One whose own scope does not reach the unit is saved with a soft warning (`unit_not_reached`). No beds-per-nurse
warning (A12).

No patient anywhere: beds, units and staff names."""
from __future__ import annotations

import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Literal

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query, Request, status
from sqlalchemy import func, select, update
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from homunculus.admin.service import CONCURRENT_CHANGE
from homunculus.admission import service, trail, unitscope
from homunculus.admission.errors import ASSIGNMENT_CONFLICT, OUT_OF_SCOPE, SHIFT_CHANGED, Refusal, field_invalid
from homunculus.admission.models import NurseBedAssignment
from homunculus.admission.schemas import (
    ShiftItem, ShiftNurse, ShiftSchedule, ShiftSchedulePut, ShiftScheduleSaved, ShiftUnit, ShiftWarning, ShiftWindow,
)
from homunculus.api.shiftclock import Shift, shift_windows
from homunculus.api.tasks import DEFAULT_SETTINGS, zone_of
from homunculus.auth.dependencies import caller_tenant_id, require_permission
from homunculus.auth.models import User
from homunculus.auth.permissions import validate_permissions
from homunculus.db import get_db
from homunculus.scope import Scope
from homunculus.tenant import scope
from homunculus.tenant.models import Bed, Room, Site, Unit

SHIFTS_MANAGE, WORKLOAD_VIEW = validate_permissions(("shifts.manage", "workload.view"))
SHIFT_ASSIGN = "shift.assign"
UNIT_NOT_FOUND = "Unit not found."
BED_NOT_FOUND = "Bed not found."

router = APIRouter(prefix="/shift-schedule", tags=["shift-schedule"])
_tenant = Depends(caller_tenant_id)


def _now() -> datetime:
    """The server's clock (a test sets it)."""
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc)


def _windows(request: Request, hospital: str, now: datetime) -> tuple[Shift, Shift]:
    """(current, next), by Görevlerim's two starts and the tenant's zone, read from the app's task store as GET /tasks
    reads them (§5 M4): the defaults (08 and 20, UTC) for a tenant that saved none, or an app without a store."""
    store = getattr(request.app.state, "tasks", None)
    settings = (store.settings(hospital) if store is not None else None) or DEFAULT_SETTINGS
    zone = zone_of(store.timezone(hospital) if store is not None else None)
    return shift_windows(now, zone, settings.shift_start_a, settings.shift_start_b)


async def _own_unit(db: AsyncSession, caller_tenant: uuid.UUID, unit_id: uuid.UUID) -> None:
    """404 unless the unit is the caller's tenant's: another tenant's is exactly a missing one (tenant/scope.py)."""
    scope.ensure_owned(await scope.tenant_of_unit(db, unit_id), caller_tenant, UNIT_NOT_FOUND)


def _in_reach(reach: Scope, unit_id: uuid.UUID) -> None:
    """403 `out_of_scope` unless the unit is one of the caller's: a unit grant, or an unrestricted caller. A bed grant
    names one bed, not its unit (D4)."""
    if not unitscope.reaches(reach, unit_id=unit_id, bed_id=None):
        raise Refusal(403, OUT_OF_SCOPE, "This unit is not one of your units.").http()


def _on_beds(columns, unit_ids: list[uuid.UUID]):
    """SELECT `columns` from the ACTIVE rows on the beds of `unit_ids` — a row is its bed's unit's (A8)."""
    return (select(*columns).select_from(NurseBedAssignment)
            .join(Bed, Bed.id == NurseBedAssignment.bed_id).join(Room, Room.id == Bed.room_id)
            .where(Room.unit_id.in_(unit_ids), NurseBedAssignment.is_active.is_(True)))


def _rows(unit_ids: list[uuid.UUID], start: datetime):
    """The active rows of the window keyed `start` on the beds of `unit_ids`, with the nurse's names."""
    return (_on_beds((NurseBedAssignment.id, NurseBedAssignment.bed_id, NurseBedAssignment.nurse_id, Room.unit_id,
                      User.full_name, User.username), unit_ids)
            .join(User, User.id == NurseBedAssignment.nurse_id)
            .where(NurseBedAssignment.shift_start == start))


def _others(columns, unit_ids: list[uuid.UUID], window: Shift):
    """The active rows of ANOTHER window that overlap `window` (A3: the shift hours changed meanwhile)."""
    return _on_beds(columns, unit_ids).where(NurseBedAssignment.shift_start != window.start,
                                             NurseBedAssignment.shift_start < window.end,
                                             NurseBedAssignment.shift_end > window.start)


async def _held(db: AsyncSession, unit_id: uuid.UUID, window: Shift) -> tuple[list, list]:
    """The unit's active rows of the window and of the windows overlapping it, read under its lock: the PUT's `before`,
    what `expected` must equal, and the rows it ends."""
    rows = list((await db.execute(_rows([unit_id], window.start))).all())
    others = list((await db.execute(_others((NurseBedAssignment.id, NurseBedAssignment.bed_id,
                                             NurseBedAssignment.nurse_id), [unit_id], window))).all())
    return rows, others


async def _schedule(db: AsyncSession, units: list[tuple[uuid.UUID, str]], window: Shift,
                    now: datetime) -> ShiftSchedule:
    """The answer for `units` (id, name), in that order, each with its active rows of the window by bed id and the count
    of the overlapping rows of another window."""
    ids = [uid for uid, _ in units]
    rows = (await db.execute(_rows(ids, window.start))).all() if ids else []
    others = dict((await db.execute(_others((Room.unit_id, func.count(NurseBedAssignment.id)), ids, window)
                                    .group_by(Room.unit_id))).all()) if ids else {}
    items: dict[uuid.UUID, list[ShiftItem]] = {}
    for _, bed_id, nurse_id, unit_id, full_name, username in rows:
        items.setdefault(unit_id, []).append(ShiftItem(bed_id=bed_id, nurse_id=nurse_id,
                                                       nurse_name=full_name or username, nurse_username=username))
    return ShiftSchedule(
        shift=ShiftWindow(start=window.start, end=window.end, which=window.which),
        units=[ShiftUnit(unit_id=uid, unit_name=name, other_rows=others.get(uid, 0),
                         items=sorted(items.get(uid, []), key=lambda item: str(item.bed_id)))
               for uid, name in units],
        generated_at=now)


async def _reaching(db: AsyncSession, people: set[uuid.UUID], caller_tenant: uuid.UUID,
                    unit_id: uuid.UUID) -> dict[uuid.UUID, bool]:
    """Whether each person's own scope reaches the unit (A10), read as the board's resolver reads it."""
    return {person: unitscope.reaches(await unitscope.caller_scope(db, person, caller_tenant), unit_id=unit_id,
                                      bed_id=None) for person in people}


def _pairs(pairs) -> list[list[str]]:
    """(bed, nurse) pairs as the audit row writes them: ids as text, sorted."""
    return sorted([str(bed), str(nurse)] for bed, nurse in pairs)


@router.get("", response_model=ShiftSchedule)
async def read_shift_schedule(
    request: Request,
    shift: Literal["current", "next"] = Query(default="current"),
    unit_id: uuid.UUID | None = Query(default=None),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user: User = Depends(require_permission(SHIFTS_MANAGE, WORKLOAD_VIEW)),
    caller_tenant: uuid.UUID = _tenant,
) -> ShiftSchedule:
    """D4: the window and the units in the caller's reach — her unit grants, every unit of her tenant when unrestricted
    — by name, each with its active rows of the window (a unit with none is listed with no item) and `other_rows`.
    `unit_id` asks for one unit: another tenant's or a missing one is a 404, one outside her units a 403 (§5 M3). Staff
    names only; no patient."""
    if unit_id is not None:
        await _own_unit(db, caller_tenant, unit_id)
    reach = await unitscope.caller_scope(db, user.id, caller_tenant)
    if unit_id is not None:
        _in_reach(reach, unit_id)
    stmt = (select(Unit.id, Unit.name).join(Site, Site.id == Unit.site_id)
            .where(Site.tenant_id == caller_tenant).order_by(Unit.name, Unit.id))
    within = unitscope.unit_clause(reach, unit_id=Unit.id)
    if within is not None:
        stmt = stmt.where(within)
    if unit_id is not None:
        stmt = stmt.where(Unit.id == unit_id)
    units = [(uid, name) for uid, name in (await db.execute(stmt)).all()]
    now = _now()
    current, following = _windows(request, await trail.hospital_of(db, caller_tenant), now)
    return await _schedule(db, units, current if shift == "current" else following, now)


@router.get("/nurses", response_model=list[ShiftNurse])
async def shift_nurses(
    unit_id: uuid.UUID = Query(...),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user: User = Depends(require_permission(SHIFTS_MANAGE)),
    caller_tenant: uuid.UUID = _tenant,
) -> list[ShiftNurse]:
    """§5 A10: whom the form offers for that unit — the tenant's active nurses and charge nurses (`service.nurse_ids`),
    whatever their grants, each with whether her own scope reaches the unit; the unit is checked as the PUT checks it.
    `name` is the full name, else the user name; sorted by it (case-folded), then the id."""
    await _own_unit(db, caller_tenant, unit_id)
    _in_reach(await unitscope.caller_scope(db, user.id, caller_tenant), unit_id)
    rows = (await db.execute(select(User.id, User.full_name, User.username)
                             .where(User.id.in_(service.nurse_ids(caller_tenant))))).all()
    reaching = await _reaching(db, {uid for uid, _, _ in rows}, caller_tenant, unit_id)
    people = [ShiftNurse(id=uid, name=full_name or username, username=username, reaches_unit=reaching[uid])
              for uid, full_name, username in rows]
    return sorted(people, key=lambda person: (person.name.casefold(), str(person.id)))


@router.put("", response_model=ShiftScheduleSaved)
async def put_shift_schedule(
    body: ShiftSchedulePut, request: Request,
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user: User = Depends(require_permission(SHIFTS_MANAGE)),
    caller_tenant: uuid.UUID = _tenant,
) -> ShiftScheduleSaved:
    """D4, §5 A1, A7, A10, M3: replace one unit's assignment for one window (the module's docstring). Every check before
    anything is written, in this order: the unit and the beds are the caller's tenant's (404); the unit is one of her
    units (403); a bed is named once, and a person in a new pair is an active member of the tenant (422); the window is
    still the current or the next one (409 `shift_changed`); then, under the unit's lock, `expected` is what holds (409),
    and every NEW pair's bed is an open bed of that unit (the bed, its room and the unit open) and its person an offered
    one (422 `field_invalid:items`). An empty `items` clears the window."""
    beds = [item.bed_id for item in body.items]
    expected = {(pair.bed_id, pair.nurse_id) for pair in body.expected}
    asked = [(item.bed_id, item.nurse_id) for item in body.items]
    await _own_unit(db, caller_tenant, body.unit_id)
    await scope.ensure_beds(db, caller_tenant, beds, BED_NOT_FOUND)
    _in_reach(await unitscope.caller_scope(db, user.id, caller_tenant), body.unit_id)
    if len(set(beds)) != len(beds):
        raise field_invalid("items", "A bed is named once.").http()
    await scope.ensure_members(db, caller_tenant, (nurse for bed, nurse in asked if (bed, nurse) not in expected))
    hospital, actor, user_id, now = await trail.hospital_of(db, caller_tenant), user.username, user.id, _now()
    window = next((w for w in _windows(request, hospital, now) if w.start == body.shift_start), None)
    if window is None:
        raise Refusal(409, SHIFT_CHANGED, "That shift is no longer the current or the next one.").http()
    # The unit's row lock (tenant/router.py `_lock_unit`), read afresh: what is read below holds until the commit.
    unit = (await db.execute(select(Unit).where(Unit.id == body.unit_id).with_for_update(key_share=True)
                             .execution_options(populate_existing=True))).scalar_one()
    unit_name, unit_open = unit.name, unit.is_active
    rows, others = await _held(db, body.unit_id, window)
    held = {(row.bed_id, row.nurse_id) for row in rows}
    if expected != held:
        await db.rollback()                       # nothing written; the lock goes with it
        raise HTTPException(status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT, detail=CONCURRENT_CHANGE)
    new = [pair for pair in asked if pair not in held]
    open_beds = set((await db.execute(
        select(Bed.id).join(Room, Room.id == Bed.room_id)
        .where(Room.unit_id == body.unit_id, Bed.is_active.is_(True), Room.is_active.is_(True)))).scalars()
    ) if unit_open else set()
    if any(bed not in open_beds for bed, _ in new):
        raise field_invalid("items", "A bed is not an open bed of this unit.").http()
    people = {nurse for _, nurse in new}
    if people and people - set((await db.execute(service.nurse_ids(caller_tenant).where(User.id.in_(people))))
                               .scalars()):
        raise field_invalid("items", "A person is not one of this hospital's nurses.").http()
    ended = [row.id for row in rows if (row.bed_id, row.nurse_id) not in set(asked)] + [row.id for row in others]
    if ended:                                     # flushed before the inserts: the index cannot be deferred
        await db.execute(update(NurseBedAssignment).where(NurseBedAssignment.id.in_(ended))
                         .values(is_active=False, closed_at=now))
    db.add_all(NurseBedAssignment(unit_id=body.unit_id, bed_id=bed, nurse_id=nurse, charge_nurse_id=user_id,
                                  shift_start=window.start, shift_end=window.end) for bed, nurse in new)
    try:
        await db.flush()
    except IntegrityError:                        # the index (bed, window) WHERE active: a coded 409, never a 500
        await db.rollback()
        raise Refusal(409, ASSIGNMENT_CONFLICT, "Another save of this window came first.").http() from None
    reaching = await _reaching(db, {nurse for _, nurse in asked}, caller_tenant, body.unit_id)
    warnings = [ShiftWarning(code="unit_not_reached", nurse_id=nurse)
                for nurse in sorted((n for n, ok in reaching.items() if not ok), key=str)]
    await db.commit()
    trail.record(request, hospital, SHIFT_ASSIGN, subject=str(body.unit_id), payload={
        "unit_id": str(body.unit_id), "shift_start": window.start.isoformat(), "shift_end": window.end.isoformat(),
        "before": _pairs(held), "after": _pairs(asked),
        "other": _pairs((row.bed_id, row.nurse_id) for row in others), "actor": actor})
    saved = await _schedule(db, [(body.unit_id, unit_name)], window, now)
    return ShiftScheduleSaved(**saved.model_dump(), warnings=warnings)
```

In `homunculus-engine/src/homunculus/admission/schemas.py` the new `ShiftSchedulePut` uses `AwareDatetime`, which the module imports already (`from pydantic import (AfterValidator, AwareDatetime, …)`).

```python
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Shift scheduling (§4.4)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
sched_router = APIRouter(prefix="/shift-schedule", tags=["shift-schedule"])


@sched_router.post("", response_model=ShiftScheduleResponse, status_code=201)
async def create_shift_schedule(
    payload: ShiftScheduleCreate,
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user: User = Depends(require_permission("shifts.manage")),
    caller_tenant: uuid.UUID = _tenant,
):
    # The unit and every assigned bed belong to the caller's tenant, and every nurse is a member
    # of it. The charge nurse is the caller, whose membership `caller_tenant_id` established.
    scope.ensure_owned(await scope.tenant_of_unit(db, payload.unit_id), caller_tenant,
                       UNIT_NOT_FOUND)
    await scope.ensure_beds(db, caller_tenant, (a.bed_id for a in payload.assignments),
                            BED_NOT_FOUND)
    await scope.ensure_members(db, caller_tenant, (a.nurse_id for a in payload.assignments))
    assignments = [a.model_dump() for a in payload.assignments]
    try:
        created, warnings = await service.create_shift_schedule(
            db, unit_id=payload.unit_id, charge_nurse_id=user.id, assignments=assignments,
        )
    except WorkflowError as exc:
        raise _wf(exc)
    await db.commit()
    for row in created:
        await db.refresh(row)
    return ShiftScheduleResponse(
        assignments=[NurseBedAssignmentResponse.model_validate(r) for r in created],
        warnings=warnings,
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Handover (§4.5, S-07, S-24; Group 2 · B3b «Vardiya devri»)
```

```python
def _shift_body(unit: uuid.UUID, bed: uuid.UUID, nurse: uuid.UUID) -> dict:
    """G4-04 (spec §5 A1, A7): PUT /shift-schedule's body — one bed to one nurse in the NEXT window of the default starts
    in UTC (the world's zone): a window still current or next even if a shift changes during the call. Nothing held."""

    return {"unit_id": unit, "shift_start": following.start.isoformat(), "items": [{"bed_id": bed, "nurse_id": nurse}],
            "expected": []}
```

## Task 5 (engine): migration `0022_workload_view`

**Files:**
- Create: `homunculus-engine/alembic/versions/0022_workload_view.py`
- Test: `homunculus-engine/tests/test_migration_0022_workload_view.py` (new; one no-DB test, three DB-backed)

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `homunculus-engine/tests/test_migration_0022_workload_view.py` with:

```python
# homunculus-engine/tests/test_migration_0022_workload_view.py
"""G4-04 migration 0022 (spec D8, §5 A8, M1, M2). Without a database: its group is the one `DEFAULT_GROUPS` gives the key
to (the charge nurse's), its index is the model's, and it follows 0020 (plan note N12: 0021 when G3-05 lands first). On
the real chain (`clean_db`, never `create_all`): `workload.view` goes to every tenant's `charge_nurse` group and to no
other, a group already holding it is left as it is; `closed_at` is added; an active row whose `unit_id` is not its bed's
unit is ended, and of the active rows of one bed and one window the newest stays and the others are ended — kept, with
`closed_at`; then one active row per bed and window holds; the downgrade drops the index, the column and the key and
re-activates NO row; 0020 → 0022 → 0020 → 0022 runs; the head is not pinned. Synthetic rows only."""
from __future__ import annotations

import importlib.util
import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from alembic import command
from sqlalchemy import inspect, text
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError

from homunculus.admission.models import NURSE_BED_ACTIVE_INDEX
from homunculus.auth.permissions import DEFAULT_GROUPS
from tests.conftest import requires_db

#: The rows migration 0022 writes, as (tenant slug, group slug).
GRANTS = ("SELECT t.slug, g.slug FROM group_permissions gp JOIN groups g ON g.id = gp.group_id "
          "JOIN tenants t ON t.id = g.tenant_id WHERE gp.permission = 'workload.view'")
CHARGE = {("h1", "charge_nurse"), ("h2", "charge_nurse")}
A = datetime(2026, 11, 12, 8, tzinfo=timezone.utc)        # a window's start


def _migration():
    path = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "alembic" / "versions" / "0022_workload_view.py"
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("migration_0022_workload_view", path)
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module


def _tenant(conn, slug: str) -> uuid.UUID:
    """A tenant with a `charge_nurse`, a `nurse` and a group of the hospital's own (`bakim_ekibi`)."""
    tid = uuid.uuid4()
    conn.execute(text("INSERT INTO tenants (id, name, slug, country_code) VALUES (:id, :s, :s, 'AZ')"),
                 {"id": tid, "s": slug})
    for group in ("charge_nurse", "nurse", "bakim_ekibi"):
        conn.execute(text("INSERT INTO groups (id, tenant_id, slug, name, description, mfa_required, is_system, "
                          "created_at, updated_at) VALUES (gen_random_uuid(), :t, :g, :g, '', false, false, now(), "
                          "now())"), {"t": tid, "g": group})
    return tid


def _bed(conn, tenant: uuid.UUID) -> tuple[uuid.UUID, uuid.UUID]:
    """A site, a unit, a room and a bed of their own: (unit, bed)."""
    site, unit, room, bed = (uuid.uuid4() for _ in range(4))
    conn.execute(text("INSERT INTO sites (id, tenant_id, name) VALUES (:id, :t, 'Ana bina')"), {"id": site, "t": tenant})
    conn.execute(text("INSERT INTO units (id, site_id, name, unit_type) VALUES (:id, :s, 'Reanimasiya', 'ADULT_ICU')"),
                 {"id": unit, "s": site})
    conn.execute(text("INSERT INTO rooms (id, unit_id, name) VALUES (:id, :u, 'Otaq 1')"), {"id": room, "u": unit})
    conn.execute(text("INSERT INTO beds (id, room_id, label) VALUES (:id, :r, '1')"), {"id": bed, "r": room})
    return unit, bed


def _user(conn, name: str) -> uuid.UUID:
    uid = uuid.uuid4()
    conn.execute(text(
        "INSERT INTO users (id, username, email, full_name, hashed_password, auth_source, totp_enabled, is_active, "
        "is_platform_support) VALUES (:id, :u, :u, 'Sentetik', 'x', 'local', false, true, false)"),
        {"id": uid, "u": f"{name}@example.com"})
    return uid


def _row(conn, unit, bed, nurse, *, start: datetime = A, hours_ago: int = 0, active: bool = True,
         row_id: uuid.UUID | None = None) -> uuid.UUID:
    """One assignment row of the 12-hour window from `start`, as the table is before 0022 (no `closed_at`). Rows made
    in one transaction with the same `hours_ago` share their `created_at` (`now()` is the transaction's); `row_id`
    fixes the id."""
    rid = row_id or uuid.uuid4()
    conn.execute(text(
        "INSERT INTO nurse_bed_assignments (id, unit_id, bed_id, nurse_id, shift_start, shift_end, is_active, "
        "created_at) VALUES (:id, :u, :b, :n, :s, :e, :a, now() - make_interval(hours => :h))"),
        {"id": rid, "u": unit, "b": bed, "n": nurse, "s": start, "e": start + timedelta(hours=12), "a": active,
         "h": hours_ago})
    return rid


def _active(conn) -> set[uuid.UUID]:
    return set(conn.execute(text("SELECT id FROM nurse_bed_assignments WHERE is_active")).scalars())


def _closed(conn) -> set[uuid.UUID]:
    return set(conn.execute(text("SELECT id FROM nurse_bed_assignments WHERE closed_at IS NOT NULL")).scalars())


def _index(conn) -> list[str]:
    return list(conn.execute(text("SELECT indexdef FROM pg_indexes WHERE indexname = :i"),
                             {"i": NURSE_BED_ACTIVE_INDEX}).scalars())


def test_the_migration_names_the_group_and_the_index_the_code_names():
    """`DEFAULT_GROUPS` seeds a tenant made later, 0022 the ones seeded before: one group, the charge nurse's (the system
    group holds every key by rule). The index is the model's. No database: the file is loaded by its path (its name
    starts with a digit)."""
    migration = _migration()
    holders = {g["slug"] for g in DEFAULT_GROUPS if "workload.view" in g["permissions"] and not g["is_system"]}
    assert holders == {migration.GROUP} == {"charge_nurse"}
    assert migration.ACTIVE_INDEX == NURSE_BED_ACTIVE_INDEX
    assert (migration.revision, migration.down_revision, migration.KEY) == ("0022", "0020", "workload.view")


@requires_db
def test_0022_gives_the_key_to_every_charge_nurse_group_and_to_no_other(clean_db):
    """ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING: a hospital that gave its charge nurses the key by hand first is not refused."""
    engine, cfg = clean_db
    command.upgrade(cfg, "0020")
    with engine.begin() as conn:
        for slug in ("h1", "h2"):
            _tenant(conn, slug)
        conn.execute(text("INSERT INTO group_permissions (group_id, permission) SELECT g.id, 'workload.view' "
                          "FROM groups g JOIN tenants t ON t.id = g.tenant_id "
                          "WHERE g.slug = 'charge_nurse' AND t.slug = 'h1'"))
    command.upgrade(cfg, "0022")
    with engine.connect() as conn:
        assert {tuple(r) for r in conn.execute(text(GRANTS))} == CHARGE
    command.upgrade(cfg, "head")                              # at 0022 or past it: a later migration may follow (M2)
    with engine.connect() as conn:
        assert {tuple(r) for r in conn.execute(text(GRANTS))} == CHARGE
        assert conn.execute(text("SELECT version_num FROM alembic_version")).scalar_one() >= "0022"


@requires_db
def test_0022_ends_a_row_of_the_wrong_unit_and_all_but_the_newest_of_a_bed_and_window_then_holds_one(clean_db):
    engine, cfg = clean_db
    command.upgrade(cfg, "0020")
    with engine.begin() as conn:
        tenant = _tenant(conn, "h1")
        (unit, bed), (unit2, bed2) = _bed(conn, tenant), _bed(conn, tenant)
        n1, n2 = _user(conn, "n1"), _user(conn, "n2")
        older = _row(conn, unit, bed, n1, hours_ago=3)                 # the older of two active rows: ended
        newest = _row(conn, unit, bed, n2, hours_ago=1)                # the newest: stays
        ended = _row(conn, unit, bed, n1, hours_ago=5, active=False)   # history already: left as it is
        later = _row(conn, unit, bed, n1, start=A + timedelta(hours=12))   # another window: its own
        astray = _row(conn, unit2, bed, n2, start=A + timedelta(hours=24))   # bed of `unit`, written under `unit2`
        # A tie on `created_at` (one transaction, one `now()`): the greater id stays.
        low = _row(conn, unit2, bed2, n1, row_id=uuid.UUID(int=1))
        high = _row(conn, unit2, bed2, n2, row_id=uuid.UUID(int=2 ** 128 - 1))
    command.upgrade(cfg, "0022")
    with engine.connect() as conn:
        assert _active(conn) == {newest, later, high}
        assert _closed(conn) == {older, astray, low}                  # the rows 0022 ended, and only those
        assert conn.execute(text("SELECT count(*) FROM nurse_bed_assignments")).scalar_one() == 7   # none deleted
        assert ended not in _active(conn) | _closed(conn)
        (definition,) = _index(conn)
        assert "UNIQUE" in definition and "(bed_id, shift_start)" in definition and "WHERE is_active" in definition
    with pytest.raises(IntegrityError), engine.begin() as conn:
        _row(conn, unit, bed, n1)                                      # a second active row of that bed and window
    with engine.begin() as conn:
        _row(conn, unit, bed, n1, active=False)                        # an ended one is history: any number of them


@requires_db
def test_the_downgrade_drops_the_index_the_column_and_the_key_and_reactivates_nothing(clean_db):
    engine, cfg = clean_db
    command.upgrade(cfg, "0020")
    with engine.begin() as conn:
        tenant = _tenant(conn, "h1")
        unit, bed = _bed(conn, tenant)
        n1 = _user(conn, "n1")
        _row(conn, unit, bed, n1, hours_ago=2)
        kept = _row(conn, unit, bed, n1, hours_ago=1)
    command.upgrade(cfg, "0022")
    with engine.begin() as conn:                              # a hospital gives the key to its own group (Roles screen)
        conn.execute(text("INSERT INTO group_permissions (group_id, permission) SELECT id, 'workload.view' "
                          "FROM groups WHERE slug = 'bakim_ekibi'"))
    command.downgrade(cfg, "0020")
    with engine.connect() as conn:
        assert {tuple(r) for r in conn.execute(text(GRANTS))} == set()
        assert _index(conn) == []
        assert "closed_at" not in {c["name"] for c in inspect(conn).get_columns("nurse_bed_assignments")}
        assert _active(conn) == {kept}                        # the ended row stays ended
        assert conn.execute(text("SELECT count(*) FROM groups")).scalar_one() == 3     # the groups stay
    command.upgrade(cfg, "0022")                              # and again
    with engine.connect() as conn:
        assert {tuple(r) for r in conn.execute(text(GRANTS))} == {("h1", "charge_nurse")}
        assert len(_index(conn)) == 1
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the migration**

Create `homunculus-engine/alembic/versions/0022_workload_view.py` with:

```python
# homunculus-engine/alembic/versions/0022_workload_view.py
"""G4-04 İş yükü (spec D2, D8, §5 A8, M1): `workload.view` for the default `charge_nurse` group («Sorumlu Hemşire») of
every tenant, and one active nurse per bed per shift window in `nurse_bed_assignments`.

Revision 0022, follows G4-03's 0020 (quality_view). G3-05's 0021 («Cihaz ölçüm kaydı») is on its own branch: whichever
of the two reaches main second sets its `down_revision` to the other's (one line, and its test's tuple), so
`alembic heads` stays one.

Upgrade, in this order:
1. `group_permissions`: `workload.view` for every group whose slug is `charge_nurse`, in every tenant (0017's, 0019's and
   0020's pattern), ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING. tests/test_migration_0022_workload_view.py holds GROUP to `DEFAULT_GROUPS`;
   a group the hospital made itself is not touched; the system group holds the key by rule.
2. `closed_at` TIMESTAMPTZ NULL (M1): when a row was ended — by this migration, then by PUT /shift-schedule.
3. An active row whose `unit_id` is not its bed's unit (the retired POST never checked it, A8) is ended.
4. Of the active rows of one bed and one `shift_start`, the newest (`created_at`, then `id`) stays and the others are
   ended. The retired POST let rows pile up.
5. `uq_nurse_bed_active_shift`: a partial unique index ON (bed_id, shift_start) WHERE is_active (the model's
   `NURSE_BED_ACTIVE_INDEX`).
"Ended" is `is_active = false, closed_at = now()`: the rows stay, as history.

IT CHANGES DATA, AND THE DOWNGRADE DOES NOT UNDO THAT (spec D8). The downgrade drops the index, the `closed_at` column
(the end times go with it) and the key from every group (a group the hospital gave it to since included: the code before
this revision has no such key); it does NOT re-activate the rows steps 3 and 4 ended — it cannot tell them from rows a
later PUT ended. Deploy notes: before the upgrade, a read-only count of the (bed_id, shift_start) pairs with more than
one active row, of the rows steps 3 and 4 would end, numbers only. The old image's exception: an api from before this
revision still has the POST, which could insert a second active row of one bed and window after 0022 and meet the
index (a 500); no client calls it — no dashboard version ever did.
ONE STATEMENT PER op.execute (asyncpg).

Revision ID: 0022
Revises: 0020
Create Date: 2026-11-12
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Sequence, Union

import sqlalchemy as sa
from alembic import op

revision: str = "0022"
down_revision: Union[str, None] = "0020"
branch_labels: Union[str, Sequence[str], None] = None
depends_on: Union[str, Sequence[str], None] = None

KEY = "workload.view"
#: The one non-system default group that holds the key (auth/permissions.py DEFAULT_GROUPS; spec D2).
GROUP = "charge_nurse"
#: admission/models.py NURSE_BED_ACTIVE_INDEX (the test holds the two to each other).
ACTIVE_INDEX = "uq_nurse_bed_active_shift"


def upgrade() -> None:
    # 1
    op.execute(f"INSERT INTO group_permissions (group_id, permission) SELECT id, '{KEY}' FROM groups "
               f"WHERE slug = '{GROUP}' ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING")
    # 2
    op.add_column("nurse_bed_assignments", sa.Column("closed_at", sa.DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True))
    # 3
    op.execute(
        "UPDATE nurse_bed_assignments AS a SET is_active = false, closed_at = now()"
        " FROM beds AS b JOIN rooms AS r ON r.id = b.room_id"
        " WHERE b.id = a.bed_id AND a.is_active AND a.unit_id <> r.unit_id")
    # 4
    op.execute(
        "UPDATE nurse_bed_assignments SET is_active = false, closed_at = now() WHERE id IN"
        " (SELECT id FROM (SELECT id, row_number() OVER (PARTITION BY bed_id, shift_start"
        " ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC) AS n FROM nurse_bed_assignments WHERE is_active) AS ranked"
        " WHERE n > 1)")
    # 5
    op.create_index(ACTIVE_INDEX, "nurse_bed_assignments", ["bed_id", "shift_start"], unique=True,
                    postgresql_where=sa.text("is_active"))


def downgrade() -> None:
    op.drop_index(ACTIVE_INDEX, table_name="nurse_bed_assignments")
    op.drop_column("nurse_bed_assignments", "closed_at")
    op.execute(f"DELETE FROM group_permissions WHERE permission = '{KEY}'")
```

```js
// G4-04 «İş yükü» (spec 2026-11-12 D6, §5): the workload page's words (strings/isyuku.js) — the spec's own Turkish,
// none of the prototype's figures that have no source, no suffix glued to a number, a time or a name, and the unit named
// in the nurse cards' and the form's titles.
import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import { DICTIONARIES } from "./catalog.js";
import { LANG_CODES } from "./langs.js";

const fill = (template, vars) => template.replace(/\{(\w+)\}/g, (_, name) => String(vars[name]));
const isyuku = (lang) => Object.entries(DICTIONARIES[lang]).filter(([key]) => key.startsWith("isyuku."));

describe("the workload page's words (isyuku)", () => {
  it("says the spec's Turkish", () => {
    const tr = DICTIONARIES.tr;
    expect([tr["isyuku.nav"], tr["isyuku.title"], tr["isyuku.motto"]])
      .toEqual(["İş yükü", "İş yükü", "Sayım, puan değil · kişiler sıralanmaz"]);
    expect(fill(tr["isyuku.shift.current"], { from: "08:00", to: "20:00" })).toBe("Bu nöbet · 08:00–20:00");
    expect(fill(tr["isyuku.shift.next"], { from: "20:00", to: "08:00" })).toBe("Sonraki nöbet · 20:00–08:00");
    expect(["colPatients", "colNurses", "colRatio", "colUnassigned", "colLate", "colRemaining", "colWaiting"]
      .map((key) => tr[`isyuku.units.${key}`])).toEqual(["Hasta", "Hemşire", "Hemşire başına hasta", "Atanmamış hasta",
      "Geciken iş", "Kalan iş", "Yanıt bekleyen alarm"]);
    expect(fill(tr["isyuku.units.ratio"], { patients: 3, nurses: 2 })).toBe("3 hasta / 2 hemşire");
    expect(tr["isyuku.units.noAssignment"]).toBe("Bu nöbet için atama yok");
    expect(tr["isyuku.units.otherRows"]).toBe("Nöbet saatleri değişti; bu nöbetin ataması yeniden yapılmalı.");
    expect(tr["isyuku.why.shiftChanged"]).toBe("nöbet değişti, yenileniyor");
    expect(tr["isyuku.nurses.note"]).toBe("Alfabetik; sıralama değildir");
    expect([tr["isyuku.form.current"], tr["isyuku.form.next"], tr["isyuku.form.save"]])
      .toEqual(["Bu nöbet", "Sonraki nöbet", "Kaydet"]);
    expect(tr["isyuku.err.conflict"]).toBe("Bu arada başka biri değiştirdi — liste yenilendi; yeniden bakın.");
    expect([tr["isyuku.form.unreached"], tr["isyuku.form.gone"]]).toEqual(["bu birimi göremez", "artık atanamaz"]);
    expect(fill(tr["isyuku.form.refused"], { reason: "x" })).toBe("Kaydedilmedi: x");
    expect(DICTIONARIES.az["isyuku.units.noAssignment"]).toBe("Bu növbə üçün təyinat yoxdur");
  });

  it("leaves out the prototype's figures that have no source", () => {
    for (const lang of LANG_CODES) {
      for (const [key, value] of isyuku(lang)) {
        expect(value, `${lang} ${key}`)
          .not.toMatch(/Yeniden dağıtım|Yenidən bölgü|перераспредел|redistribut|yüksek risk|yüksək risk|высокий риск|high risk|saat başına|по часам|per hour|ortalamanın|× от среднего|the mean ×/i);
      }
    }
  });

  it("glues no suffix to a number, a time or a name in Turkish or Azerbaijani", () => {
    for (const lang of ["tr", "az"]) {
      for (const [key, value] of isyuku(lang)) {
        expect(value, `${lang} ${key}`).not.toMatch(/\{\w+\}[^\s·;,.:)–]/);
      }
    }
  });

  it("names the unit in the nurse cards' and the form's titles, in every language", () => {
    for (const lang of LANG_CODES) {
      for (const key of ["isyuku.nurses.title", "isyuku.form.title"]) {
        expect(DICTIONARIES[lang][key], `${lang} ${key}`).toContain("{unit}");
      }
    }
  });
});
```

```js
// G4-04 «İş yükü» (screens/isyuku/, spec 2026-11-12 D6 and §5): who looks after which bed this shift, and how much
```

```js
  it("reads the shift's assignment with an authed GET /shift-schedule?shift=, one unit when asked, and the form's people", async () => {
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the calls, and the fake API's mocks**

In `dashboard/src/backendApi.js`, find:

```js
    qualityIndicators(days = 7) { return authed(`/quality?days=${encodeURIComponent(days)}`); },
```

Replace with:

```js
    qualityIndicators(days = 7) { return authed(`/quality?days=${encodeURIComponent(days)}`); },
    // ---- G4-04: «İş yükü» (homunculus-engine admission/shifts.py, /shift-schedule) ----
    /** The window («current» holds now, «next» follows it) and, per unit in my reach (one unit with `unitId`), its
     *  active nurse–bed rows and the count of another window's overlapping ones: {shift: {start, end, which}, units:
     *  [{unit_id, unit_name, items: [{bed_id, nurse_id, nurse_name, nurse_username}], other_rows}], generated_at}. Staff
     *  names, never a patient. */
    shiftSchedule(shift = "current", unitId = null) {
      const q = new URLSearchParams({ shift });
      if (unitId) q.set("unit_id", unitId);
      return authed(`/shift-schedule?${q}`);
    },
    /** [{id, name, username, reaches_unit}] — the people the assignment form offers for that unit: the tenant's active
     *  nurses and charge nurses, each with whether her own scope reaches the unit. */
    shiftNurses(unitId) { return authed(`/shift-schedule/nurses?unit_id=${encodeURIComponent(unitId)}`); },
    /** Replace one unit's assignment for one window: {unit_id, shift_start (the GET's shift.start), items: [{bed_id,
     *  nurse_id}], expected: [...] (the rows read, required)} → the unit's new state and `warnings` ([{code:
     *  "unit_not_reached", nurse_id}]). 409 `shift_changed` (`err.apiCode`): the window is no longer current or next;
     *  409 concurrentChange or `assignment_conflict`: someone saved since `expected` was read. */
    saveShiftSchedule(body) { return authed("/shift-schedule", { method: "PUT", body }); },
```

## Task 9 (dashboard): the pure `isyuku.js` and the synthetic ward

**Files:**
- Create: `dashboard/src/live/screens/isyuku/isyuku.testutil.js`

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the synthetic ward**

Create `dashboard/src/live/screens/isyuku/isyuku.testutil.js` with:

```js
// Test-only: a synthetic ward for «İş yükü» (G4-04) — two open units and a closed one (GET /tenant/structure), the
// shift's assignment (GET /shift-schedule, also for one unit), the form's people, the task queue (GET /tasks) and the
// live board (GET /patients), as the API sends them. Synthetic names and ids only; no screen may print a patient's
// name, a patient id or a UUID.
import { alarm, board, entry, identity } from "../../data/board.testutil.js";
import { BED, NEO, REA, SITE, T } from "../kabul/kabul.testutil.js";

export { BED, NEO, REA };
/** A closed unit: the page leaves it out. */
export const OLD = "33333333-3333-4333-8333-333333333309";
export const NURSE = Object.freeze({
  aysel: "66666666-6666-4666-8666-666666666601", leyla: "66666666-6666-4666-8666-666666666602",
  zaur: "66666666-6666-4666-8666-666666666603", nigar: "66666666-6666-4666-8666-666666666604",
});
export const PATIENT_NAME = "Ayşe Sentetik";
/** 10:00 UTC on 12 November: inside SHIFT. */
export const NOW_MS = Date.parse("2026-11-12T10:00:00Z");

const bed = (n, label, state = "AVAILABLE", isActive = true) => ({ id: BED(n), label, state, is_active: isActive });
const room = (n, name, beds) => ({ id: `44444444-4444-4444-8444-4444444444${n}`, name, room_number: null,
  is_isolation: false, is_active: true, beds });

/** Reanimasiya: Otaq 1 — beds 1 and 2 occupied, 3 leaving (PENDING_DISCHARGE: still a patient), 4 free; Otaq 2 — bed 5
 *  occupied, bed 6 closed. Neonatal: N-1 — N1 occupied, N2 free. «Köhnə şöbə»: closed. */
export function tree() {
  return { tenant_id: T, sites: [{ id: SITE, name: "Ana bina", is_active: true, units: [
    { id: REA, name: "Reanimasiya", unit_type: "ADULT_ICU", is_active: true, rooms: [
      room(11, "Otaq 1", [bed(1, "1", "OCCUPIED"), bed(2, "2", "OCCUPIED"), bed(3, "3", "PENDING_DISCHARGE"), bed(4, "4")]),
      room(12, "Otaq 2", [bed(5, "5", "OCCUPIED"), bed(6, "6", "AVAILABLE", false)]),
    ] },
    { id: NEO, name: "Neonatal", unit_type: "NICU", is_active: true, rooms: [
      room(13, "N-1", [bed(11, "N1", "OCCUPIED"), bed(12, "N2")]),
    ] },
    { id: OLD, name: "Köhnə şöbə", unit_type: "ADULT_ICU", is_active: false, rooms: [] },
  ] }] };
}

/** The current shift (A, 08:00–20:00 UTC on 12 November) and the next (B). */
export const SHIFT = Object.freeze({ start: "2026-11-12T08:00:00Z", end: "2026-11-12T20:00:00Z", which: "A" });
export const NEXT = Object.freeze({ start: "2026-11-12T20:00:00Z", end: "2026-11-13T08:00:00Z", which: "B" });
const item = (n, nurse, name) => ({ bed_id: BED(n), nurse_id: NURSE[nurse], nurse_name: name,
  nurse_username: `${nurse}@sentetik.example` });

/** GET /shift-schedule: Reanimasiya's beds 1 and 2 to Leyla, 5 to Aysel — and the closed bed 6 to Zaur, a row the page
 *  does not count; Neonatal has none. */
export function schedule(over = {}) {
  return { shift: SHIFT, generated_at: "2026-11-12T10:00:00Z", units: [
    { unit_id: NEO, unit_name: "Neonatal", items: [], other_rows: 0 },
    { unit_id: REA, unit_name: "Reanimasiya", other_rows: 0, items: [item(1, "leyla", "Leyla Sentetik"),
      item(2, "leyla", "Leyla Sentetik"), item(5, "aysel", "Aysel Sentetik"), item(6, "zaur", "Zaur Sentetik")] },
  ], ...over };
}

/** GET /shift-schedule for one unit: «current» as `schedule()` has it, «next» with no row yet. */
export function unitSchedule(shift, unitId) {
  const whole = schedule();
  const unit = whole.units.find((u) => u.unit_id === unitId);
  return { ...whole, shift: shift === "next" ? NEXT : SHIFT,
    units: unit ? [{ ...unit, items: shift === "next" ? [] : unit.items }] : [] };
}

/** GET /shift-schedule/nurses: the tenant's active nurses and charge nurses, by name — Nigar's own grants do not reach
 *  Reanimasiya; Zaur is no longer offered (he holds the closed bed 6 still). */
export const NURSES = Object.freeze([
  { id: NURSE.aysel, name: "Aysel Sentetik", username: "aysel@sentetik.example", reaches_unit: true },
  { id: NURSE.leyla, name: "Leyla Sentetik", username: "leyla@sentetik.example", reaches_unit: true },
  { id: NURSE.nigar, name: "Nigar Sentetik", username: "nigar@sentetik.example", reaches_unit: false },
]);

const task = (id, due, over = {}) => ({ id, patient_id: "", title: "Sentetik görev", due_at: due, target_role: "nurse",
  state: "open", late: false, created_by: "hem.ayse", created_at: "2026-11-12T07:00:00Z", claimed_by: null,
  claimed_at: null, closed_by: null, closed_at: null, ...over });

/** GET /tasks over the week that ends with the shift: S-101 (bed 1) — a late open task, an open one due in the shift, a
 *  done one, and a late vital; S-102 (bed 2) — an open one due in the shift; S-105 (bed 5) — an open one due after the
 *  shift; S-111 (Neonatal's N1) — an open one due in the shift, and a vital due (not late). */
export function tasks() {
  return { from: "2026-11-05T20:00:00Z", to: SHIFT.end, now: "2026-11-12T10:00:00Z",
    shift: { start: SHIFT.start, end: NEXT.end }, settings: { grace_min: 30, intervals: {} },
    patients: [
      { patient_id: "S-101", bed_key: null, unit_id: REA, vital: { state: "late" }, tasks: [
        task("t1", "2026-11-12T09:00:00Z", { late: true }), task("t2", "2026-11-12T15:00:00Z"),
        task("t3", "2026-11-12T09:30:00Z", { state: "done" })] },
      { patient_id: "S-102", bed_key: null, unit_id: REA, vital: null, tasks: [task("t6", "2026-11-12T18:00:00Z")] },
      { patient_id: "S-105", bed_key: null, unit_id: REA, vital: null, tasks: [task("t4", "2026-11-12T21:00:00Z")] },
      { patient_id: "S-111", bed_key: null, unit_id: NEO, vital: { state: "due" }, tasks: [task("t5", "2026-11-12T12:00:00Z")] },
    ] };
}

const card = (id, n, unitId, alarms = []) => entry(id, alarms,
  { identity: identity({ patient_id: id, display_name: PATIENT_NAME, unit_id: unitId, bed_id: BED(n) }) });
/** GET /patients: S-101 on bed 1 with a critical alarm, S-102 on bed 2, S-105 on bed 5 with a high one, S-111 on N1. */
export const BOARD = board([
  card("S-101", 1, REA, [alarm("septic_shock", "critical")]), card("S-102", 2, REA),
  card("S-105", 5, REA, [alarm("news2", "high")]), card("S-111", 11, NEO),
]);
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing test**

Create `dashboard/src/live/screens/isyuku/isyuku.test.js` with:

```js
// isyuku.test.js — G4-04 «İş yükü» (spec D6, §5 A4–A6, A10, A11, A13), the pure half: the round's read (the shift, then
// /tasks over its week) and its two sources (a shift that has ended is «nöbet değişti»); the rows (the tree's open
// units) with each cell from its sources or why not — the reach, the units the GET lists, «Bu nöbet için atama yok»
// only from a fresh listing; «Geciken iş» and «Kalan iş»; the nurse cards (alphabetical, beds, patients and «Kalan iş»
// only, found through the board) and the unassigned beds; the form's beds, people and body. Synthetic ward
// (isyuku.testutil.js); the board in the adapter's shape, as ozet.test.js writes it.
import { describe, it, expect, vi } from "vitest";
import { CONNECTION } from "../../../liveConnection.js";
import { WHY, noSource, okSource } from "../ozet/ozet.js";
import {
  ALL, SHIFT_CHANGED, boardWork, formModel, isNursesAnswer, isScheduleAnswer, labelled, meanOf, readWork, remainingOf,
  saveBody, workSources, workloadPage, workloadRights,
} from "./isyuku.js";
import { BED, NEO, NOW_MS, NURSE, NURSES, REA, schedule, tasks, tree, unitSchedule } from "./isyuku.testutil.js";

const PATIENTS = [{ patient_id: "S-101", unitId: REA, bedId: BED(1) }, { patient_id: "S-102", unitId: REA, bedId: BED(2) },
  { patient_id: "S-105", unitId: REA, bedId: BED(5) }, { patient_id: "S-111", unitId: NEO, bedId: BED(11) }];
const alarm = (patientId, kind, severity, over = {}) => ({
  id: `${patientId}::${kind}`, patientId, kind, severity, ack: false, status: "FIRED", downgraded: false, ...over,
});
const ALERTS = [alarm("S-101", "septic_shock", "critical"), alarm("S-105", "news2", "high"),
  alarm("S-105", "acute_change", "moderate", { ack: true, status: "ACKNOWLEDGED" })];      // answered: not waiting
const LIVE = { connection: CONNECTION.LIVE, patients: PATIENTS, alerts: ALERTS, nowMs: NOW_MS };
const EVERY = okSource({ all: true, units: null });
const RIGHTS = workloadRights(["workload.view", "shifts.manage", "tasks.view", "board.view", "admissions.view"]);
const read = (data) => ({ phase: "ok", data, atMs: NOW_MS, error: null });

function sources(over = {}) {
  return { tree: tree(), occupancy: okSource(tree()), reach: EVERY, wholeTree: false, schedule: okSource(schedule()),
    tasks: okSource(tasks()), board: boardWork(LIVE, true), ...over };
}
const page = (over = {}, options = {}) => workloadPage(sources(over), { lang: "az", ...options });
const cellsOf = (p) => Object.fromEntries(p.rows.map((row) => [row.name, row.cells]));

describe("the round's read and its sources", () => {
  it("reads the current shift, then /tasks over the week that ends with it", async () => {
    const api = { shiftSchedule: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(schedule()), tasks: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(tasks()) };
    expect(await readWork(api, true)).toEqual({ schedule: schedule(), tasks: { ok: true, data: tasks() } });
    expect(api.shiftSchedule).toHaveBeenCalledWith("current");
    expect(api.tasks).toHaveBeenCalledWith("2026-11-05T20:00:00.000Z", "2026-11-12T20:00:00Z");
    api.tasks.mockClear();
    expect(await readWork(api, false)).toEqual({ schedule: schedule(), tasks: null });
    expect(api.tasks).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("keeps the schedule when the task read fails, and says why for the tasks alone", async () => {
    const refused = Object.assign(new Error("no"), { status: 403 });
    const api = { shiftSchedule: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(schedule()), tasks: vi.fn().mockRejectedValue(refused) };
    const work = await readWork(api, true);
    expect(work.tasks).toEqual({ ok: false, error: refused });
    const { schedule: s, tasks: t } = workSources(read(work), RIGHTS, NOW_MS);
    expect([s.ok, t]).toEqual([true, noSource(WHY.noRight)]);
  });

  it("takes the shift's answer only whole, and calls one whose shift has ended «nöbet değişti» — tasks too", () => {
    expect(isScheduleAnswer(schedule())).toBe(true);
    for (const bad of [null, { ...schedule(), shift: { ...schedule().shift, which: "C" } }, { ...schedule(), generated_at: "x" },
      schedule({ units: [{ unit_id: REA, unit_name: "Reanimasiya", other_rows: 0, items: [{ bed_id: BED(1), nurse_id: 7 }] }] }),
      schedule({ units: [{ unit_id: REA, unit_name: "Reanimasiya", other_rows: -1, items: [] }] })]) {
      expect(isScheduleAnswer(bad)).toBe(false);
    }
    const fresh = workSources(read({ schedule: schedule(), tasks: { ok: true, data: tasks() } }), RIGHTS, NOW_MS);
    expect([fresh.schedule.ok, fresh.tasks.ok]).toEqual([true, true]);
    const after = Date.parse("2026-11-12T20:00:00Z");
    expect(workSources(read({ schedule: schedule(), tasks: { ok: true, data: tasks() } }), RIGHTS, after))
      .toEqual({ schedule: noSource(SHIFT_CHANGED), tasks: noSource(SHIFT_CHANGED) });
    const short = { ...tasks(), patients: [{ patient_id: "S-1", unit_id: REA, vital: null, tasks: [{ id: "t", late: false }] }] };
    expect(workSources(read({ schedule: schedule(), tasks: { ok: true, data: short } }), RIGHTS, NOW_MS).tasks)
      .toEqual(noSource(WHY.failed));
    expect(isNursesAnswer(NURSES)).toBe(true);
    expect(isNursesAnswer([{ id: "n-1", name: "x" }])).toBe(false);
  });

  it("asks for the shift with either key, and reads /tasks only with tasks.view", () => {
    expect(workloadRights(["shifts.manage"])).toMatchObject({ schedule: true, manage: true, tasks: false });
    expect(workloadRights(["workload.view"])).toMatchObject({ schedule: true, manage: false });
    expect(workloadRights(["summary.view"]).schedule).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("the units for this shift", () => {
  it("joins every source per open unit of the tree, in its order — a closed unit is left out", () => {
    const p = page();
    expect(p.rows.map((row) => row.name)).toEqual(["Reanimasiya", "Neonatal"]);
    expect(p.shift).toEqual({ startMs: Date.parse("2026-11-12T08:00:00Z"), endMs: Date.parse("2026-11-12T20:00:00Z"), which: "A" });
    expect(cellsOf(p).Reanimasiya).toEqual({
      patients: { value: 4 },                       // 1, 2, 3 (leaving: still a patient) and 5; never the closed bed
      nurses: { value: 2 },                         // Leyla and Aysel; Zaur's row is on a closed bed
      ratio: { patients: 3, nurses: 2 },            // the occupied beds that have a nurse: 1, 2, 5
      unassigned: { value: 1 },                     // bed 3
      late: { value: 2 },                           // the late hand task and the late vital (§5 A4)
      remaining: { value: 2 },                      // open, not late, due before the shift ends: t2 and t6 — not t4
      waiting: { value: 2 },                        // the critical and the high; the answered one is not
    });
    expect(meanOf(3, 2)).toBe(1.5);
    expect(p.otherRows).toBe(false);
  });

  it("says a listed unit has no assignment this shift — never «0 hemşire» — and still counts its unassigned patients", () => {
    expect(cellsOf(page()).Neonatal).toEqual({
      patients: { value: 1 }, nurses: { none: true }, ratio: { none: true }, unassigned: { value: 1 },
      late: { value: 0 }, remaining: { value: 1 }, waiting: { value: 0 },
    });
  });

  it("counts «Kalan iş» from open hand tasks not late, due before the shift ends", () => {
    const end = Date.parse("2026-11-12T20:00:00Z");
    const at = (due, over = {}) => ({ tasks: [{ state: "open", late: false, due_at: due, ...over }] });
    expect(remainingOf(at("2026-11-12T19:59:00Z"), end)).toBe(1);
    expect(remainingOf(at("2026-11-12T20:00:00Z"), end)).toBe(0);
    expect(remainingOf(at("2026-11-12T09:00:00Z", { late: true }), end)).toBe(0);
    expect(remainingOf(at("2026-11-12T12:00:00Z", { state: "done" }), end)).toBe(0);
  });

  it("says so when the shift hours changed meanwhile (other windows' rows)", () => {
    const s = schedule();
    s.units[1].other_rows = 2;
    expect(page({ schedule: okSource(s) }).otherRows).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe("Decision 18: every number is real or says why not (§5 A6)", () => {
  it("a unit the GET does not list: «yetki yok» in its assignment cells — never «atama yok»", () => {
    const s = schedule({ units: [schedule().units[1]] });          // Neonatal not listed
    const neo = cellsOf(page({ schedule: okSource(s) })).Neonatal;
    expect([neo.nurses, neo.ratio, neo.unassigned]).toEqual(Array(3).fill({ why: WHY.noRight }));
    expect([neo.patients, neo.late, neo.waiting]).toEqual([{ value: 1 }, { value: 0 }, { value: 0 }]);
  });

  it("a unit outside her reach: «yetki yok» in every counted cell, its beds too unless the tree is whole", () => {
    const reach = okSource({ all: false, units: new Set([REA.toLowerCase()]) });
    const neo = cellsOf(page({ reach, schedule: okSource(schedule({ units: [schedule().units[1]] })) })).Neonatal;
    expect(Object.values(neo)).toEqual(Array(7).fill({ why: WHY.noRight }));
    const whole = cellsOf(page({ reach, wholeTree: true, schedule: okSource(schedule({ units: [schedule().units[1]] })) })).Neonatal;
    expect(whole.patients).toEqual({ value: 1 });
  });

  it("a missing or ended shift: the assignment cells, the tasks and the cards say why", () => {
    for (const why of [WHY.noRight, SHIFT_CHANGED]) {
      const p = page({ schedule: noSource(why), tasks: noSource(why) });
      expect(p.shift).toBeNull();
      const rea = cellsOf(p).Reanimasiya;
      expect([rea.nurses, rea.ratio, rea.unassigned, rea.late, rea.remaining]).toEqual(Array(5).fill({ why }));
      expect([rea.patients, rea.waiting]).toEqual([{ value: 4 }, { value: 2 }]);
      expect([p.cards, p.cardsWhy, p.shiftWhy]).toEqual([null, why, why]);
    }
  });

  it("a board that is not LIVE: the alarm column and the cards' «Kalan iş» say why (§5 A5)", () => {
    const board = boardWork({ ...LIVE, connection: CONNECTION.DEGRADED }, true);
    expect(board).toEqual(noSource(WHY.stale));
    const p = page({ board });
    expect(cellsOf(p).Reanimasiya.waiting).toEqual({ why: WHY.stale });
    expect(cellsOf(p).Reanimasiya.remaining).toEqual({ value: 2 });      // the unit's own: /tasks names its unit
    const leyla = p.cards.find((card) => card.label === "Leyla Sentetik");
    expect([leyla.patients, leyla.tasks]).toEqual([{ value: 2 }, { why: WHY.stale }]);
  });

  it("a tree held from an older read whose latest failed: the patients, the ratio and the unassigned say why", () => {
    const p = page({ occupancy: noSource(WHY.failed) });
    const rea = cellsOf(p).Reanimasiya;
    expect([rea.patients, rea.ratio, rea.unassigned]).toEqual(Array(3).fill({ why: WHY.failed }));
    expect(rea.nurses).toEqual({ value: 2 });
    expect(p.unassigned).toEqual({ why: WHY.failed });
  });

  it("no rows without a tree — the tree's own reason", () => {
    expect(page({ tree: null, occupancy: noSource(WHY.loading) })).toMatchObject({ rows: null, why: WHY.loading });
    expect(page({ tree: null, occupancy: noSource(WHY.noRight) })).toMatchObject({ rows: null, why: WHY.noRight });
  });
});

describe("the nurse cards (§5 A5, A11, A13)", () => {
  it("one card per nurse with a bed there, alphabetical: her beds, her patients and their «Kalan iş» — nothing else", () => {
    const { cards } = page();
    expect(cards).toEqual([
      { key: NURSE.aysel, label: "Aysel Sentetik", beds: ["5 · Otaq 2 · Reanimasiya"], patients: { value: 1 }, tasks: { value: 0 } },
      { key: NURSE.leyla, label: "Leyla Sentetik", beds: ["1 · Otaq 1 · Reanimasiya", "2 · Otaq 1 · Reanimasiya"],
        patients: { value: 2 }, tasks: { value: 2 } },
    ]);
  });

  it("then the occupied beds nobody has; one unit chosen narrows both, a choice no listed row holds falls back to all", () => {
    expect(page().unassigned).toEqual({ beds: ["3 · Otaq 1 · Reanimasiya", "N1 · N-1 · Neonatal"] });
    const neo = page({}, { selected: NEO.toLowerCase() });
    expect([neo.selected, neo.cards, neo.unassigned]).toEqual([NEO.toLowerCase(), [], { beds: ["N1 · N-1 · Neonatal"] }]);
    expect(page({}, { selected: "a unit the rows do not hold" }).selected).toBe(ALL);
  });

  it("tells two of one name apart by their user names, and sorts in the reader's language", () => {
    expect(labelled([{ key: "1", name: "Leyla", username: "leyla.a" }, { key: "2", name: "Leyla", username: "leyla.b" },
      { key: "3", name: "Aysel", username: "aysel" }]).map((p) => p.label)).toEqual(["Leyla (leyla.a)", "Leyla (leyla.b)", "Aysel"]);
    const s = schedule();
    s.units[1].items[2] = { ...s.units[1].items[2], nurse_name: "Leyla Sentetik" };           // Aysel's row, renamed
    expect(page({ schedule: okSource(s) }).cards.map((card) => card.label))
      .toEqual(["Leyla Sentetik (aysel@sentetik.example)", "Leyla Sentetik (leyla@sentetik.example)"]);
  });
});

describe("the form's model and its body (§5 A7, A10)", () => {
  const rea = () => page().rows.find((row) => row.name === "Reanimasiya");

  it("one picker per open bed, the rows read chosen; the people by name, one who cannot see the unit marked", () => {
    const model = formModel({ row: rea(), schedule: unitSchedule("current", REA), nurses: [...NURSES], lang: "az" });
    expect(model.beds.map((bed) => [bed.label, bed.held])).toEqual([
      ["1 · Otaq 1 · Reanimasiya", NURSE.leyla], ["2 · Otaq 1 · Reanimasiya", NURSE.leyla],
      ["3 · Otaq 1 · Reanimasiya", ""], ["4 · Otaq 1 · Reanimasiya", ""], ["5 · Otaq 2 · Reanimasiya", NURSE.aysel]]);
    expect(model.beds[2].people.map((person) => [person.label, person.note]))
      .toEqual([["Aysel Sentetik", null], ["Leyla Sentetik", null], ["Nigar Sentetik", "unreached"]]);
    expect(model.expected).toEqual([{ bed_id: BED(1), nurse_id: NURSE.leyla }, { bed_id: BED(2), nurse_id: NURSE.leyla },
      { bed_id: BED(5), nurse_id: NURSE.aysel }, { bed_id: BED(6), nurse_id: NURSE.zaur }]);    // the closed bed's row too
  });

  it("offers a person no longer offered only on the bed she holds, marked", () => {
    const answer = unitSchedule("current", REA);
    answer.units[0].items.push({ bed_id: BED(4), nurse_id: NURSE.zaur, nurse_name: "Zaur Sentetik", nurse_username: "zaur@sentetik.example" });
    const model = formModel({ row: rea(), schedule: answer, nurses: [...NURSES], lang: "az" });
    const four = model.beds.find((bed) => bed.label.startsWith("4 "));
    expect(four.people.map((person) => [person.label, person.note]).at(-1)).toEqual(["Zaur Sentetik", "gone"]);
    expect(model.beds[0].people.map((person) => person.key)).not.toContain(NURSE.zaur);
  });

  it("sends the window's key, the chosen pairs, and every row read as `expected`", () => {
    const model = formModel({ row: rea(), schedule: unitSchedule("current", REA), nurses: [...NURSES], lang: "az" });
    const choices = new Map(model.beds.map((bed) => [bed.key, bed.held]));
    choices.set(model.beds[2].key, NURSE.nigar);
    expect(saveBody(model, choices)).toEqual({ unit_id: REA, shift_start: "2026-11-12T08:00:00Z",
      items: [{ bed_id: BED(1), nurse_id: NURSE.leyla }, { bed_id: BED(2), nurse_id: NURSE.leyla },
        { bed_id: BED(3), nurse_id: NURSE.nigar }, { bed_id: BED(5), nurse_id: NURSE.aysel }],
      expected: model.expected });
  });
});
```
