- [ ] **Step 4: Write `isyuku.js`**

Create `dashboard/src/live/screens/isyuku/isyuku.js` with:

```js
// src/live/screens/isyuku/isyuku.js — G4-04 «İş yükü» (spec docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-12-g4-is-yuku-design.md D6,
// §5), pure: the page's sources once Decision 18 is applied, the per-unit join — the structure × the shift's nurse–bed
// assignment × the task queue × the live board — and the assignment form's model. IsYukuScreen.jsx only words it.
//
// THE READ. One round (useSummaryReads' `extra`): GET /shift-schedule for the current window, then /tasks over the week
// that ends with that shift (`from` = its end − 7 days, `to` = its end: the widest range /tasks takes, §5 A4) — so a task
// already late at the shift change is counted and tomorrow's are not. An answer whose shift has ended is «nöbet
// değişti, yenileniyor» until the next read, and so is the task read made over it (A6).
//
// THE ROWS are the tree's open units (a closed unit has no open bed: no patient, nothing to assign). A CELL is a count
// from its sources, or why not (ozet.js WHY, and this page's SHIFT_CHANGED):
//   «Hasta» — the tree (Doluluk's occupied beds: OCCUPIED and the two pending states, open beds only); «yetki yok» for a
//     unit outside the caller's reach unless the tree is the whole hospital's (`structure.manage`), as on the summary;
//   «Hemşire», «Hemşire başına hasta», «Atanmamış hasta» — the assignment (its rows on the unit's open beds only) and the
//     tree; «yetki yok» for a unit the GET does not list (out of her unit reach); «Bu nöbet için atama yok» only from a
//     fresh answer that lists the unit with no row;
//   «Geciken iş» — open hand tasks the server marks late and a vital task in state late (ozet.js `lateOf`); «Kalan iş» —
//     open hand tasks NOT late, due before the shift ends; vital tasks are not in it (A4); both by the card's unit;
//   «Yanıt bekleyen alarm» — the live board's `needsAnswer`, LIVE only (ozet.js `boardSource`);
//   a cell of the tasks or the board is «yetki yok» for a unit outside her reach (GET /scope/me), as on the summary.
//
// THE NURSE CARDS, for the selection (one listed unit, or all): one per nurse with a row on an open bed there,
// alphabetical in the reader's language (two of one name told apart by their user names, A11) — her beds by their
// structure name, her patients (the occupied ones) and «Kalan iş» of those patients; no late work and no alarm count
// (A13: late work belongs to the unit). /tasks names no bed, so a card's patients are found through the live board — an
// assigned bed → the board card on it → that patient's /tasks entry (A5); without a LIVE board the cell says why. Then
// the occupied beds nobody has («Atanmamış»).
//
// Counts only: no score, no target ratio. No patient's name or id: beds by their structure name, nurses by name.
import { bedLabel, indexStructure, normId } from "../../data/structure.js";
import { waitingAlarms } from "../../data/boardStore.js";
import { withOpen } from "../doluluk/occupancy.js";
import { WHY, boardSource, isTasksAnswer, lateOf, noSource, okSource, readSource, rightsOf } from "../ozet/ozet.js";

/** The selection «Tüm birimler». A unit's own key is its id (normId). */
export const ALL = "*";
/** This page's own reason (§5 A6): the answer's shift has ended; the next read is coming. */
export const SHIFT_CHANGED = "shiftChanged";
/** The widest range GET /tasks takes (api/tasks.py MAX_WINDOW). */
export const TASKS_SPAN_MS = 7 * 24 * 60 * 60_000;
/** The beds a patient is in: OCCUPIED and the two pending states (doluluk/occupancy.js `unitCounts`' occupied). */
const WITH_PATIENT = new Set(["OCCUPIED", "PENDING_DISCHARGE", "PENDING_TRANSFER_OUT"]);

const isObject = (value) => value !== null && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value);
const listOf = (value) => (Array.isArray(value) ? value : []);
const isCount = (value) => Number.isInteger(value) && value >= 0;

function msOf(iso) {
  const ms = typeof iso === "string" ? Date.parse(iso) : NaN;
  return Number.isFinite(ms) ? ms : null;
}

/** GET /shift-schedule as this page reads it: the window, the server's time, and units each with an id, a name, a count
 *  of other windows' rows and items each naming a bed and a nurse. Anything else is a failed read. */
export function isScheduleAnswer(answer) {
  return isObject(answer) && isObject(answer.shift) && msOf(answer.shift.start) !== null
    && msOf(answer.shift.end) !== null && (answer.shift.which === "A" || answer.shift.which === "B")
    && msOf(answer.generated_at) !== null && Array.isArray(answer.units)
    && answer.units.every((u) => isObject(u) && typeof u.unit_id === "string" && typeof u.unit_name === "string"
      && isCount(u.other_rows) && Array.isArray(u.items) && u.items.every((i) => isObject(i)
        && typeof i.bed_id === "string" && typeof i.nurse_id === "string" && typeof i.nurse_name === "string"
        && typeof i.nurse_username === "string"));
}

/** GET /shift-schedule/nurses: [{id, name, username, reaches_unit}]. */
export const isNursesAnswer = (answer) => Array.isArray(answer) && answer.every((person) => isObject(person)
  && typeof person.id === "string" && typeof person.name === "string" && typeof person.username === "string"
  && typeof person.reaches_unit === "boolean");

/** The round's read: the current shift, then — when the session may (`tasks.view`) — /tasks over the week ending with
 *  it. A failed task read keeps the schedule (its own reason, `tasks.ok: false`); a session that ended is not
 *  swallowed. */
export async function readWork(api, mayTasks) {
  const schedule = await api.shiftSchedule("current");
  if (!mayTasks || !isScheduleAnswer(schedule)) return { schedule, tasks: null };
  const end = schedule.shift.end;
  try {
    return { schedule, tasks: { ok: true, data: await api.tasks(new Date(Date.parse(end) - TASKS_SPAN_MS).toISOString(), end) } };
  } catch (error) {
    if (error?.sessionEnded) throw error;
    return { schedule, tasks: { ok: false, error } };
  }
}
/** The two forms of the read useSummaryReads takes as `extra.read` (module constants: a new function each render would
 *  read again each render). */
export const readWorkAndTasks = (api) => readWork(api, true);
export const readWorkAlone = (api) => readWork(api, false);
export const isWorkAnswer = (answer) => isScheduleAnswer(answer?.schedule);

/** What the page's sources need, by the API's own rules: the summary's (ozet.js `rightsOf`) without the waiting
 *  handovers; the shift's read for either key; the form for `shifts.manage`. */
export function workloadRights(perms) {
  const has = (key) => Array.isArray(perms) && perms.includes(key);
  return { ...rightsOf(perms), pending: false, schedule: has("workload.view") || has("shifts.manage"),
    manage: has("shifts.manage") };
}

/** The task answer for «Geciken iş» and «Kalan iş»: G4-02's shape (ozet.js `isTasksAnswer`) and each hand task's state
 *  and due time. */
const isWorkTasks = (answer) => isTasksAnswer(answer) && answer.patients.every((patient) => patient.tasks
  .every((task) => typeof task.state === "string" && msOf(task.due_at) !== null));

/** The round's read (`readSource`-shaped, from useSummaryReads' `extra`) as the page's two sources at `nowMs`: the
 *  schedule, and the task queue read over it. An answer whose shift has ended is SHIFT_CHANGED, both of them (A6). */
export function workSources(read, rights, nowMs) {
  const work = readSource(read, rights.schedule);
  const noTasks = noSource(WHY.noRight);
  if (!work.ok) return { schedule: work, tasks: rights.tasks ? work : noTasks };
  const { schedule, tasks } = work.data;
  if (msOf(schedule.shift.end) <= nowMs) {
    const ended = noSource(SHIFT_CHANGED);
    return { schedule: ended, tasks: rights.tasks ? ended : noTasks };
  }
  let taskSource = noTasks;
  if (rights.tasks && tasks?.ok) taskSource = isWorkTasks(tasks.data) ? okSource(tasks.data, work.atMs) : noSource(WHY.failed);
  else if (rights.tasks) taskSource = noSource(tasks?.error?.status === 403 ? WHY.noRight : WHY.failed);
  return { schedule: okSource(schedule, work.atMs), tasks: taskSource };
}

/** The live board as the page counts it: ozet.js `boardSource` — LIVE only, its waiting alarms by the unit of their
 *  card — and the patients on each bed (the board card's `bedId`): how a nurse card finds its patients (A5). */
export function boardWork(board, mayRead) {
  const base = boardSource(board, mayRead);
  if (!base.ok) return base;
  const onBed = new Map();
  for (const patient of listOf(board.patients)) {
    if (!patient?.bedId) continue;
    const key = normId(patient.bedId);
    onBed.set(key, [...(onBed.get(key) ?? []), patient.patient_id]);
  }
  return okSource({ byUnit: base.data.byUnit, onBed });
}

/** One patient's «Kalan iş»: open hand tasks NOT late, due before the shift ends at `endMs` (A4). */
export const remainingOf = (patient, endMs) => listOf(patient?.tasks).filter((task) => {
  const due = msOf(task?.due_at);
  return task?.state === "open" && task.late !== true && due !== null && due < endMs;
}).length;

/** «Hemşire başına hasta»'s mean, to one decimal: 3 patients, 2 nurses → 1.5. */
export const meanOf = (patients, nurses) => Math.round((patients / nurses) * 10) / 10;

/** A11: people as shown — `label` is the name, and the user name in brackets beside it when another of the same list
 *  bears the same name. */
export function labelled(people) {
  const count = new Map();
  for (const person of people) count.set(person.name, (count.get(person.name) ?? 0) + 1);
  return people.map((person) => ({ ...person,
    label: count.get(person.name) > 1 ? `${person.name} (${person.username})` : person.name }));
}

/** One order everywhere (A11): the reader's language's collation of the label, then the key. */
export const byLabel = (lang) => (a, b) => a.label.localeCompare(b.label, lang) || (a.key < b.key ? -1 : a.key > b.key ? 1 : 0);

/** The open units of the tree, in its order, each with its open beds: key, id, structure name («1 · Otaq 1 ·
 *  Reanimasiya»), whether a patient is in it. */
function unitsOf(tree) {
  const index = indexStructure(tree);
  const out = [];
  for (const site of listOf(tree?.sites)) {
    for (const unit of listOf(site?.units)) {
      if (unit?.id == null || unit.is_active === false) continue;
      const beds = [];
      for (const room of listOf(unit.rooms)) {
        for (const raw of listOf(room?.beds)) {
          const bed = withOpen(unit, room, raw);
          if (!bed.open || bed.id == null) continue;
          beds.push({ key: normId(bed.id), id: bed.id, label: bedLabel(index, bed.id), occupied: WITH_PATIENT.has(bed.state) });
        }
      }
      out.push({ key: normId(unit.id), id: unit.id, name: unit.name ?? "", beds });
    }
  }
  return out;
}

/** The page. `tree`: the last tree held (null: none read yet); `occupancy`: the tree's own source (ozet.js
 *  `structureSource`); `reach`: the caller's (ozet.js `reachSource`, GET /scope/me); `wholeTree`: the tree is the whole
 *  hospital's (`structure.manage`); `schedule`, `tasks` (`workSources`), `board` (`boardWork`): sources. `selected`: ALL
 *  or a listed unit's key — one the rows no longer hold falls back to ALL; `lang`: the cards' collation.
 *
 *  { shift: {startMs, endMs, which} | null, shiftWhy, rows: [{key, id, name, beds, listed, items, cells}] | null, why,
 *    otherRows, selected, choices: [row], cards: [{key, label, beds, patients, tasks}] | null, cardsWhy,
 *    unassigned: {beds: [name]} | {why} | null } */
export function workloadPage({ tree, occupancy, reach, wholeTree = false, schedule, tasks, board }, { selected = ALL, lang } = {}) {
  const shift = schedule.ok ? { startMs: msOf(schedule.data.shift.start), endMs: msOf(schedule.data.shift.end),
    which: schedule.data.shift.which } : null;
  const head = { shift, shiftWhy: schedule.ok ? null : schedule.why };
  if (!tree) {
    const why = occupancy.ok ? WHY.loading : occupancy.why;
    return { ...head, rows: null, why, otherRows: false, selected: ALL, choices: [], cards: null, cardsWhy: why, unassigned: null };
  }

  // The units the GET lists, each with its rows by bed.
  const listed = new Map();
  if (schedule.ok) {
    for (const unit of schedule.data.units) {
      listed.set(normId(unit.unit_id), { otherRows: unit.other_rows, items: new Map(unit.items.map((i) => [normId(i.bed_id),
        { key: normId(i.nurse_id), name: i.nurse_name, username: i.nurse_username }])) });
    }
  }
  // «Geciken iş» and «Kalan iş» by the card's unit; «Kalan iş» by patient, for the cards.
  const late = new Map();
  const remaining = new Map();
  const remainingOfPatient = new Map();
  if (tasks.ok && shift) {
    for (const patient of tasks.data.patients) {
      const unit = normId(patient.unit_id);
      const n = remainingOf(patient, shift.endMs);
      late.set(unit, (late.get(unit) ?? 0) + lateOf(patient));
      remaining.set(unit, (remaining.get(unit) ?? 0) + n);
      remainingOfPatient.set(patient.patient_id, n);
    }
  }
  const outOf = (key) => reach.ok && !reach.data.all && !reach.data.units.has(key);
  const gate = (key, cell) => (outOf(key) ? { why: WHY.noRight } : !reach.ok ? { why: reach.why } : cell);

  const rows = unitsOf(tree).map((unit) => {
    const mine = listed.get(unit.key);
    const items = mine ? unit.beds.filter((bed) => mine.items.has(bed.key)).map((bed) => ({ bed, ...mine.items.get(bed.key) })) : [];
    const nurses = new Set(items.map((i) => i.key));
    const assigned = new Set(items.map((i) => i.bed.key));
    const occupied = unit.beds.filter((bed) => bed.occupied);
    let patients = { value: occupied.length };
    if (!wholeTree && outOf(unit.key)) patients = { why: WHY.noRight };
    else if (!occupancy.ok) patients = { why: occupancy.why };
    else if (!wholeTree && !reach.ok) patients = { why: reach.why };
    let held = null;                                            // why the assignment cells cannot be counted
    if (!schedule.ok) held = { why: schedule.why };
    else if (!mine) held = { why: WHY.noRight };                // out of her unit reach: the GET does not list it
    const withPatients = held ?? ("why" in patients ? patients : null);
    return {
      key: unit.key, id: unit.id, name: unit.name, beds: unit.beds, listed: Boolean(mine), items,
      cells: {
        patients,
        nurses: held ?? (nurses.size === 0 ? { none: true } : { value: nurses.size }),
        ratio: withPatients ?? (nurses.size === 0 ? { none: true }
          : { patients: occupied.filter((bed) => assigned.has(bed.key)).length, nurses: nurses.size }),
        unassigned: withPatients ?? { value: occupied.filter((bed) => !assigned.has(bed.key)).length },
        late: gate(unit.key, tasks.ok ? { value: late.get(unit.key) ?? 0 } : { why: tasks.why }),
        remaining: gate(unit.key, tasks.ok ? { value: remaining.get(unit.key) ?? 0 } : { why: tasks.why }),
        waiting: gate(unit.key, board.ok ? { value: board.data.byUnit.get(unit.key)?.total ?? 0 } : { why: board.why }),
      },
    };
  });

  const choices = rows.filter((row) => row.listed);
  const pick = selected !== ALL && choices.some((row) => row.key === selected) ? selected : ALL;
  const otherRows = [...listed.values()].some((unit) => unit.otherRows > 0);
  const page = { ...head, rows, why: null, otherRows, selected: pick, choices };
  if (!schedule.ok) return { ...page, cards: null, cardsWhy: schedule.why, unassigned: null };

  const shown = choices.filter((row) => pick === ALL || row.key === pick);
  const byNurse = new Map();
  for (const row of shown) {
    for (const { bed, key, name, username } of row.items) {
      const card = byNurse.get(key) ?? { key, name, username, beds: [] };
      card.beds.push(bed);
      byNurse.set(key, card);
    }
  }
  const tasksOf = (beds) => beds.reduce((sum, bed) => sum + (board.data.onBed.get(bed.key) ?? [])
    .reduce((n, patient) => n + (remainingOfPatient.get(patient) ?? 0), 0), 0);
  const cards = labelled([...byNurse.values()]).map((card) => ({
    key: card.key, label: card.label, beds: card.beds.map((bed) => bed.label),
    patients: occupancy.ok ? { value: card.beds.filter((bed) => bed.occupied).length } : { why: occupancy.why },
    tasks: !tasks.ok ? { why: tasks.why } : !board.ok ? { why: board.why } : { value: tasksOf(card.beds) },
  })).sort(byLabel(lang));
  const unassigned = occupancy.ok
    ? { beds: shown.flatMap((row) => row.beds.filter((bed) => bed.occupied && !row.items.some((i) => i.bed.key === bed.key))
      .map((bed) => bed.label)) }
    : { why: occupancy.why };
  return { ...page, cards, cardsWhy: null, unassigned };
}

/** «Nöbet ataması»'s model for one row (a listed unit) from the form's own reads — `schedule` (GET for that unit and
 *  window) and `nurses` (GET …/nurses): one picker per open bed, its held nurse ("" for none) and its people — the
 *  offered ones (one whose scope does not reach the unit marked "unreached") and, on the bed she holds only, a person no
 *  longer offered (marked "gone", A10) — by label in the reader's language; `expected`: every row the read returned,
 *  a closed bed's and a no-longer-offered nurse's included (A7). */
export function formModel({ row, schedule, nurses, lang }) {
  const unit = schedule.units[0] ?? { items: [], other_rows: 0 };
  const offered = new Set(nurses.map((person) => person.id));
  const gone = new Map(unit.items.filter((i) => !offered.has(i.nurse_id))
    .map((i) => [i.nurse_id, { id: i.nurse_id, name: i.nurse_name, username: i.nurse_username }]));
  const everyone = labelled([...nurses.map((person) => ({ key: person.id, name: person.name, username: person.username,
    note: person.reaches_unit ? null : "unreached" })),
  ...[...gone.values()].map((person) => ({ key: person.id, name: person.name, username: person.username, note: "gone" }))]);
  const order = byLabel(lang);
  const heldOn = new Map(unit.items.map((i) => [normId(i.bed_id), i.nurse_id]));
  return {
    unitId: row.id, shiftStart: schedule.shift.start, shift: schedule.shift, otherRows: unit.other_rows,
    expected: unit.items.map((i) => ({ bed_id: i.bed_id, nurse_id: i.nurse_id })),
    noneOffered: nurses.length === 0,
    beds: row.beds.map((bed) => {
      const held = heldOn.get(bed.key) ?? "";
      return { key: bed.key, id: bed.id, label: bed.label, held,
        people: everyone.filter((person) => person.note !== "gone" || person.key === held).sort(order) };
    }),
  };
}

/** The PUT's body (§5 A1, A7): the window's key, the pickers with a nurse, and every row the form read. */
export function saveBody(model, choices) {
  return {
    unit_id: model.unitId, shift_start: model.shiftStart,
    items: model.beds.map((bed) => ({ bed_id: bed.id, nurse_id: choices.get(bed.key) ?? "" })).filter((item) => item.nurse_id),
    expected: model.expected,
  };
}
```

// IsYukuScreen.test.jsx — «İş yükü» (G4-04, spec D6 and §5): one h1, the shift and no patient anywhere; the units table
// from its sources, «Bu nöbet için atama yok»; the nurse cards (alphabetical, beds, patients, «Kalan iş») and the
// unassigned beds; a unit chosen; Decision 18 (a board not LIVE, a refused assignment, an ended shift, a failed queue);
// «Nöbet ataması» for `shifts.manage` — one picker per open bed, a save with the window's key and `expected`, the soft
// warning by name, a 409 read again, the next shift, a refusal keeping the choices — and none for a viewer. The
// default language is az. A fixed clock; synthetic ward (isyuku.testutil.js).
import React from "react";
import { describe, it, expect, afterEach, beforeEach, vi } from "vitest";
import { act, cleanup, fireEvent, screen, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import { hhmm } from "../../i18n/dates.js";
import { mount, refusal } from "../../session/mount.testutil.jsx";
import { profile, token } from "../../session/testTokens.testutil.js";
import { StructureProvider } from "../../data/StructureProvider.jsx";
import { PERMISSION_KEYS } from "../admin/catalogue.js";
import { SCOPE_ADMIN } from "../admin/admin.testutil.js";
import { expectNoUuid } from "../kabul/kabul.testutil.js";
import { IsYukuScreen } from "./IsYukuScreen.jsx";
import {
  BED, BOARD, NEXT, NOW_MS, NURSE, NURSES, PATIENT_NAME, REA, SHIFT, schedule, tasks, tree, unitSchedule,
} from "./isyuku.testutil.js";

const ADMIN = [...PERMISSION_KEYS];
const settle = () => act(async () => { await new Promise((resolve) => { setTimeout(resolve, 0); }); });

/** Mount the page for a session holding `perms`, the board LIVE unless `live` is false (its stream never opens). */
async function mountIsYuku({ perms = ADMIN, setup, live = true } = {}) {
  const mounted = await mount(<StructureProvider><IsYukuScreen /></StructureProvider>, {
    access: token({ perms }), me: profile({ perms }),
    setup: (api) => {
      api.structure.mockResolvedValue(tree());
      api.boardPatients.mockResolvedValue(BOARD);
      api.streamTicket.mockResolvedValue({ ticket: "t-1" });
      api.tasks.mockResolvedValue(tasks());
      api.myScope.mockResolvedValue(SCOPE_ADMIN);
      api.shiftSchedule.mockImplementation((shift = "current", unitId = null) =>
        Promise.resolve(unitId ? unitSchedule(shift, unitId) : schedule()));
      api.shiftNurses.mockResolvedValue([...NURSES]);
      setup?.(api);
    },
  });
  await settle();
  await act(async () => { await mounted.board.start(); });
  if (live) await act(async () => { mounted.stream.open(); await new Promise((resolve) => { setTimeout(resolve, 0); }); });
  await settle();
  return mounted;
}

beforeEach(() => { vi.setSystemTime(NOW_MS); });
afterEach(() => { cleanup(); vi.useRealTimers(); });

/** The units table's body rows, each as its cells' text. */
const unitRows = () => within(screen.getByRole("table", { name: "Şöbələrə görə iş yükü, bu növbə" })).getAllByRole("row")
  .slice(1).map((row) => [...row.querySelectorAll("th, td")].map((c) => c.textContent));
const nursesBox = () => document.querySelector("[data-nurses]");
const cardNames = () => [...nursesBox().querySelectorAll("[data-nurse] h3")].map((h) => h.textContent);
const formBox = () => document.querySelector("[data-form]");
const pick = async (name) => {
  const select = screen.getByLabelText("Şöbə");
  const option = [...select.options].find((o) => o.textContent === name);
  await act(async () => { fireEvent.change(select, { target: { value: option.value } }); });
  await settle();
};
const bedLabels = () => [...formBox().querySelectorAll("label")].map((label) => label.textContent);
const save = async () => {
  await act(async () => { fireEvent.click(within(formBox()).getByRole("button", { name: "Yadda saxla" })); });
  await settle();
};

describe("the workload page", () => {

    await settle();

    expect(within(formBox()).getByLabelText("1 · Otaq 1 · Reanimasiya")).toHaveValue("");
    await save();

  it("is drawn only when the structure was read; and not at all for a viewer without shifts.manage", async () => {
    let mounted = await mountIsYuku({ perms: ADMIN.filter((key) => key !== "shifts.manage") });
    await pick("Reanimasiya");

    act(() => { mounted.board.clear(); });

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the screen**

Create `dashboard/src/live/screens/isyuku/IsYukuScreen.jsx` with:

```jsx
// src/live/screens/isyuku/IsYukuScreen.jsx — G4-04 «İş yükü» (spec docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-12-g4-is-yuku-design.md,
// D6 and §5), for `workload.view` or `shifts.manage`. Top to bottom: the header (the shift, «HH:MM itibarıyla», «Sayım,
// puan değil · kişiler sıralanmaz», «Yenile»), the units table for this shift, the unit picker with the nurse cards
// (alphabetical; beds, patients, «Kalan iş») and the unassigned beds, and — with `shifts.manage` only — «Nöbet ataması»
// for the chosen unit. The rules are isyuku.js's; this file words them. Every number is real or a «—» with its reason
// (Decision 18). No patient's name or id anywhere. Read on open, on «Yenile» and every 60 s while visible: G4-02's reads
// (useSummaryReads) with GET /shift-schedule and /tasks over its shift in the same round; the form reads its own unit
// and window.
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useI18n } from "../../i18n/I18nProvider.jsx";
import { hhmm } from "../../i18n/dates.js";
import { useSession } from "../../session/SessionProvider.jsx";
import { useBoard } from "../../data/BoardProvider.jsx";
import { useStructure } from "../../data/StructureProvider.jsx";
import { useSnoozeClock } from "../../data/useSnoozeClock.js";
import { readErrorText } from "../../apiErrors.js";
import { Alert, Box, Button, Field, Note, Seg, Select, Table } from "../../ui/index.js";
import { WHY, reachSource, readSource, structureSource } from "../ozet/ozet.js";
import { useSummaryReads } from "../ozet/useSummaryReads.js";
import {
  ALL, SHIFT_CHANGED, boardWork, formModel, isNursesAnswer, isScheduleAnswer, isWorkAnswer, meanOf, readWorkAlone,
  readWorkAndTasks, saveBody, workSources, workloadPage, workloadRights,
} from "./isyuku.js";

const COLUMNS = Object.freeze([
  "isyuku.units.colUnit", "isyuku.units.colPatients", "isyuku.units.colNurses", "isyuku.units.colRatio",
  "isyuku.units.colUnassigned", "isyuku.units.colLate", "isyuku.units.colRemaining", "isyuku.units.colWaiting",
]);

/** A number with one decimal as the reader writes it: a decimal comma in tr, az and ru, a point in en. */
const decimal = (lang, n) => (lang === "en" ? n.toFixed(1) : n.toFixed(1).replace(".", ","));

/** «—» and why (ozet's `Missing`, with this page's own reason, «nöbet değişti»). */
function Gap({ t, why }) {
  const key = why === SHIFT_CHANGED ? "isyuku.why.shiftChanged" : `ozet.why.${why}`;
  return (
    <span data-missing={why}>
      <span className="font-data font-bold">—</span>{" "}
      <span className="text-12 font-normal text-mute">{t(key)}</span>
    </span>
  );
}
const whyText = (t, why) => t(why === SHIFT_CHANGED ? "isyuku.why.shiftChanged" : `ozet.why.${why}`);

/** A cell's text, or «—» and why. */
const figure = (t, cell, render = (c) => c.value) => ("why" in cell ? <Gap t={t} why={cell.why} /> : render(cell));

function ShiftLine({ t, page }) {
  if (!page.shift) return <p className="m-0 text-13 font-semibold" data-shift>{t("isyuku.shift.label")} <Gap t={t} why={page.shiftWhy} /></p>;
  return (
    <p className="m-0 text-13 font-semibold" data-shift>
      {t("isyuku.shift.current", { from: hhmm(page.shift.startMs), to: hhmm(page.shift.endMs) })}
    </p>
  );
}

function UnitRow({ t, lang, row }) {
  const { patients, nurses, ratio, unassigned, late, remaining, waiting } = row.cells;
  const ratioText = (c) => (c.none ? "—" : `${t("isyuku.units.ratio", { patients: c.patients, nurses: c.nurses })} · ${
    t("isyuku.units.mean", { mean: decimal(lang, meanOf(c.patients, c.nurses)) })}`);
  return (
    <tr>
      <th scope="row">{row.name}</th>
      <td className="font-data">{figure(t, patients)}</td>
      <td className="font-data">{figure(t, nurses, (c) => (c.none ? t("isyuku.units.noAssignment") : c.value))}</td>
      <td className="font-data">{figure(t, ratio, ratioText)}</td>
      <td className="font-data">{figure(t, unassigned)}</td>
      <td className="font-data">{figure(t, late)}</td>
      <td className="font-data">{figure(t, remaining)}</td>
      <td className="font-data">{figure(t, waiting)}</td>
    </tr>
  );
}

function UnitsBox({ t, lang, page }) {
  let body;
  if (page.rows === null) {
    body = page.why === WHY.loading ? <p className="m-0 text-13 text-mute">{t("isyuku.units.loading")}</p>
      : <Note tone="warn">{t("isyuku.units.missing", { why: whyText(t, page.why) })}</Note>;
  } else if (page.rows.length === 0) {
    body = <p className="m-0 text-13 text-mute">{t("isyuku.units.none")}</p>;
  } else {
    body = (
      <>
        {page.otherRows ? <Note tone="warn">{t("isyuku.units.otherRows")}</Note> : null}
        <Table caption={t("isyuku.units.caption")}>
          <thead><tr>{COLUMNS.map((key) => <th key={key} scope="col">{t(key)}</th>)}</tr></thead>
          <tbody>{page.rows.map((row) => <UnitRow key={row.key} t={t} lang={lang} row={row} />)}</tbody>
        </Table>
        <p className="m-0 text-12 text-mute">{t("isyuku.units.note")}</p>
      </>
    );
  }
  return (
    <Box title={t("isyuku.units.title")} icon="building" level={2}>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-2" data-units>{body}</div>
    </Box>
  );
}

function NurseCard({ t, card }) {
  const line = (key, value) => <p className="m-0 text-12.5"><span className="text-mute">{t(key)}: </span>{value}</p>;
  return (
    <li className="flex min-w-[220px] flex-1 flex-col gap-1 rounded-box border border-line bg-card px-[14px] py-3" data-nurse>
      <h3 className="m-0 font-text text-13 font-semibold">{card.label}</h3>
      {line("isyuku.nurses.beds", card.beds.join(", "))}
      {line("isyuku.nurses.patients", figure(t, card.patients))}
      {line("isyuku.nurses.tasks", figure(t, card.tasks))}
    </li>
  );
}

function Unassigned({ t, unassigned }) {
  let body;
  if ("why" in unassigned) body = <Gap t={t} why={unassigned.why} />;
  else if (unassigned.beds.length === 0) body = <span className="text-mute">{t("isyuku.nurses.unassignedNone")}</span>;
  else body = unassigned.beds.join(", ");
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-1" data-unassigned>
      <h3 className="m-0 font-text text-13 font-semibold">{t("isyuku.nurses.unassigned")}</h3>
      <p className="m-0 text-13">{body}</p>
    </div>
  );
}

function NursesBox({ t, page, onSelect }) {
  const chosen = page.choices.find((row) => row.key === page.selected);
  let body;
  if (page.cards === null) body = <Note tone="warn">{t("isyuku.nurses.missing", { why: whyText(t, page.cardsWhy) })}</Note>;
  else {
    body = (
      <>
        <p className="m-0 text-12 font-semibold">{t("isyuku.nurses.note")}</p>
        {page.cards.length === 0 ? <p className="m-0 text-13 text-mute">{t("isyuku.nurses.none")}</p> : (
          <ul className="m-0 flex list-none flex-wrap gap-2.5 p-0">
            {page.cards.map((card) => <NurseCard key={card.key} t={t} card={card} />)}
          </ul>
        )}
        <Unassigned t={t} unassigned={page.unassigned} />
      </>
    );
  }
  return (
    <Box title={t("isyuku.nurses.title", { unit: chosen ? chosen.name : t("isyuku.pick.all") })} icon="users" level={2}>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-2" data-nurses>
        {page.choices.length > 0 ? (
          <Field label={t("isyuku.pick.label")} layout="col" className="max-w-[320px]">
            <Select value={page.selected} onChange={(event) => onSelect(event.target.value)}>
              <option value={ALL}>{t("isyuku.pick.all")}</option>
              {page.choices.map((row) => <option key={row.key} value={row.key}>{row.name}</option>)}
            </Select>
          </Field>
        ) : null}
        {body}
      </div>
    </Box>
  );
}

/** The form's two reads — the unit's rows of the window and the people offered — on open, on a change of window, and on
 *  `reload` (after a save, a 409, «Yeniden dene»). The newest read wins. */
function useAssignment(store, unitId, which) {
  const [state, setState] = useState({ phase: "loading", schedule: null, nurses: null, error: null });
  const [attempt, setAttempt] = useState(0);
  const reload = useCallback(() => { setAttempt((n) => n + 1); }, []);
  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;
    setState({ phase: "loading", schedule: null, nurses: null, error: null });
    Promise.all([store.track(() => store.api.shiftSchedule(which, unitId)), store.track(() => store.api.shiftNurses(unitId))])
      .then(([schedule, nurses]) => {
        if (!alive) return;
        setState(isScheduleAnswer(schedule) && isNursesAnswer(nurses)
          ? { phase: "ok", schedule, nurses, error: null } : { phase: "failed", schedule: null, nurses: null, error: null });
      }, (error) => { if (alive) setState({ phase: "failed", schedule: null, nurses: null, error }); });
    return () => { alive = false; };
  }, [store, unitId, which, attempt]);
  return { ...state, reload };
}

function Outcome({ t, outcome }) {
  if (!outcome) return null;
  if (outcome.kind === "saved") {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
        <Note tone="info">{t("isyuku.form.saved")}</Note>
        {outcome.warnings.map((w) => (
          <Note key={w.nurse_id} tone="warn">{t("isyuku.form.warnUnreached", { name: w.name ?? t("isyuku.form.someone") })}</Note>
        ))}
      </div>
    );
  }
  const words = { conflict: t("isyuku.err.conflict"), shiftChanged: t("isyuku.err.shiftChanged"),
    refused: outcome.error ? t("isyuku.form.refused", { reason: readErrorText(t, outcome.error) }) : null };
  return <Alert tone="crit"><p className="m-0">{words[outcome.kind]}</p></Alert>;
}

/** «Nöbet ataması» for one listed unit (the parent keys it by the unit, so another unit starts afresh). Drawn only when
 *  its two reads and the structure have answered (§5 A6); the choices are built from the read once and kept — the poll
 *  does not rebase a form being edited (A7). */
function AssignmentBox({ t, lang, store, row, structure, onSaved }) {
  const [which, setWhich] = useState("current");
  const read = useAssignment(store, row.id, which);
  const [choices, setChoices] = useState(null);       // bed key → nurse id ("": nobody)
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [outcome, setOutcome] = useState(null);
  const model = useMemo(() => (read.phase === "ok" ? formModel({ row, schedule: read.schedule, nurses: read.nurses, lang }) : null),
    // Only a new read rebuilds the model: a poll that redraws the beds must not undo what she picked.
    [read.phase, read.schedule, read.nurses, lang]); // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  useEffect(() => { setChoices(model ? new Map(model.beds.map((bed) => [bed.key, bed.held])) : null); }, [model]);

  const save = async () => {
    if (saving || !model || !choices) return;
    setSaving(true);
    setOutcome(null);
    const names = new Map(model.beds.flatMap((bed) => bed.people).map((person) => [person.key, person.name]));
    try {
      const answer = await store.track(() => store.api.saveShiftSchedule(saveBody(model, choices)));
      const warnings = Array.isArray(answer?.warnings) ? answer.warnings : [];
      setOutcome({ kind: "saved", warnings: warnings.map((w) => ({ ...w, name: names.get(w.nurse_id) ?? null })) });
      read.reload();
      onSaved();
    } catch (error) {
      if (error?.status === 409) {
        setOutcome({ kind: error.apiCode === "shift_changed" ? "shiftChanged" : "conflict" });
        read.reload();
        onSaved();
      } else setOutcome({ kind: "refused", error });
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  let body;
  if (!structure.ok) body = <Note tone="warn">{t("isyuku.form.missing", { why: whyText(t, structure.why) })}</Note>;
  else if (read.phase === "loading" || (read.phase === "ok" && choices === null)) {
    body = <p className="m-0 text-13 text-mute">{t("isyuku.form.loading")}</p>;
  } else if (read.phase === "failed") {
    body = (
      <Alert tone="crit">
        <p className="m-0">{read.error ? t("isyuku.form.failed", { reason: readErrorText(t, read.error) }) : t("isyuku.form.unreadable")}</p>
        <Button size="sm" onClick={read.reload}>{t("admin.retry")}</Button>
      </Alert>
    );
  } else if (model.beds.length === 0) {
    body = <p className="m-0 text-13 text-mute">{t("isyuku.form.noBeds")}</p>;
  } else {
    const note = (person) => (person.note ? ` — ${t(`isyuku.form.${person.note}`)}` : "");
    body = (
      <>
        <p className="m-0 text-13 font-semibold">
          {t(`isyuku.shift.${which}`, { from: hhmm(Date.parse(model.shift.start)), to: hhmm(Date.parse(model.shift.end)) })}
        </p>
        {model.otherRows > 0 ? <Note tone="warn">{t("isyuku.units.otherRows")}</Note> : null}
        {model.noneOffered ? <Note tone="warn">{t("isyuku.form.noNurses")}</Note> : null}
        <div className="grid gap-x-3 min-[861px]:grid-cols-2">
          {model.beds.map((bed) => (
            <Field key={bed.key} label={bed.label} layout="col">
              <Select value={choices.get(bed.key) ?? ""}
                onChange={(event) => { const next = new Map(choices); next.set(bed.key, event.target.value); setChoices(next); }}>
                <option value="">{t("isyuku.form.empty")}</option>
                {bed.people.map((person) => <option key={person.key} value={person.key}>{`${person.label}${note(person)}`}</option>)}
              </Select>
            </Field>
          ))}
        </div>
        <div><Button variant="primary" size="sm" busy={saving} onClick={save}>{t("isyuku.form.save")}</Button></div>
      </>
    );
  }
  const options = [{ value: "current", label: t("isyuku.form.current") }, { value: "next", label: t("isyuku.form.next") }];
  return (
    <Box title={t("isyuku.form.title", { unit: row.name })} icon="edit" level={2}
      right={<Seg label={t("isyuku.form.which")} options={options} value={which}
        onChange={(next) => { setWhich(next); setOutcome(null); }} />}>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-2" data-form>
        {body}
        <Outcome t={t} outcome={outcome} />
      </div>
    </Box>
  );
}

export function IsYukuScreen() {
  const { t, lang } = useI18n();
  const { user, store } = useSession();
  const board = useBoard();
  const boardNowMs = useSnoozeClock();
  const tree = useStructure();
  const rights = useMemo(() => workloadRights(user?.perms), [user?.perms]);
  // G4-02's round without its own /tasks read (this page reads /tasks over the shift, in `extra`) or the handovers.
  const summaryRights = useMemo(() => ({ structure: rights.structure, tasks: false, pending: false }), [rights.structure]);
  const extra = useMemo(() => ({ may: rights.schedule, read: rights.tasks ? readWorkAndTasks : readWorkAlone,
    valid: isWorkAnswer }), [rights.schedule, rights.tasks]);
  const reads = useSummaryReads({ store, rights: summaryRights, reloadStructure: tree.reload, extra });
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState(ALL);
  const occupancy = structureSource(tree, rights.structure);
  const { schedule, tasks } = workSources(reads.extra, rights, Date.now());
  const page = workloadPage({
    tree: tree.known ? tree.structure : null, occupancy, reach: reachSource(readSource(reads.scope, true)),
    wholeTree: rights.wholeTree, schedule, tasks,
    board: boardWork({ connection: board.connection, patients: board.patients, alerts: board.alerts, nowMs: boardNowMs },
      rights.board),
  }, { selected, lang });
  const chosen = page.choices.find((row) => row.key === page.selected) ?? null;
  const formable = rights.manage && page.choices.length > 0;

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-0 max-w-[1200px] flex-col gap-3">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start gap-3">
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <h1 className="m-0 font-display text-23 font-bold">{t("isyuku.title")}</h1>
          <ShiftLine t={t} page={page} />
          <p className="m-0 text-13 text-mute" data-as-of>
            {reads.fullAt ? t("ozet.asOf", { time: hhmm(reads.fullAt) }) : t("ozet.noFullRead")}
          </p>
          <p className="m-0 text-13 font-semibold">{t("isyuku.motto")}</p>
        </div>
        <Button size="sm" icon="clock" busy={reads.busy} onClick={reads.refresh}>{t("ozet.refresh")}</Button>
      </div>
      <p className="m-0 text-13 text-mute">{t("isyuku.intro")}</p>
      <UnitsBox t={t} lang={lang} page={page} />
      <NursesBox t={t} page={page} onSelect={setSelected} />
      {formable && chosen ? (
        <AssignmentBox key={chosen.key} t={t} lang={lang} store={store} row={chosen} structure={occupancy}
          onSaved={reads.refresh} />
      ) : null}
      {formable && !chosen ? (
        <Box title={t("isyuku.form.title", { unit: t("isyuku.pick.all") })} icon="edit" level={2}>
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-2" data-form><p className="m-0 text-13 text-mute">{t("isyuku.form.pickUnit")}</p></div>
        </Box>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

### 3.26 «İş yükü»: bu nöbet kim hangi yatağa bakıyor (Grup 4 · G4-04)

- **Ne:** Sol raydaki **«İş yükü»** sayfası (`/is-yuku`; Azerbaycanca da «İş yükü», Rusça «Нагрузка», İngilizce
  «Workload»). İki iş görür: sorumlu hemşire nöbetin başında hemşireleri yataklara atar (**«Nöbet ataması»**), ve sayfa
  her birim için bu nöbetin iş yükünü sayar. «Sayım, puan değil · kişiler sıralanmaz»: puan, ölçek ya da hedef oran
  yoktur; geciken iş birime yazılır, kişiye yazılmaz. Hiçbir yerde hasta adı ya da kimliği yoktur; yataklar adlarıyla,
  hemşireler adlarıyla yazılır. Örneklerdeki adlar ve sayılar uydurmadır.
- **Durum:** kodda (`feat/g4-is-yuku`); main'e ve hastane sunucusuna sahibin sözüyle çıkar. Sunucuda göç `0022`
  çalışmalıdır.
- **Kim görür, kim atar:** sayfayı yeni yetki `workload.view` («“İş yükü” sayfasını açar …») ya da `shifts.manage`
  açar; atama formu yalnız `shifts.manage` ile görünür. `shifts.manage`'ın açıklaması bu dilimde değişti: eskiden «Nöbet
  çizelgesi oluşturur», şimdi «Nöbet atamasını yapar: bu ve sonraki nöbet için, birim birim, hemşireleri yataklara
  atar». Varsayılan **«Sorumlu Hemşire»** grubu ikisini de taşır (göç `0022` var olan hastanelere `workload.view`'ı
  verir; sonra kurulan bir hastanenin grubu kendiliğinden taşır); Sistem Yöneticisi her yetkiyi kural gereği taşır;
  başka bir gruba Yönetim › Roller ve yetkiler'den kodsuz verilebilir. Bir çalışma ekranıdır: bu iki yetkiden yalnız
  birini taşıyan bir hesap bununla oturum açar ve girişte bu sayfaya düşer. Rayda «Kalite göstergeleri»nden sonra gelir;
  kimsenin giriş sayfası değişmedi (Sorumlu Hemşire yine «Yataklar»a düşer). Sayıların her biri kendi kaynağının
  yetkisini ister: görevler `tasks.view`, alarmlar ve hemşire kartlarının «Kalan iş»i `board.view`, yataklar bir kabul ya
  da yapı yetkisi; olmayan yerde «— yetki yok» yazar.
- **Nöbet:** Görevlerim'in iki nöbet başlangıcı (Yönetim › Görevler; varsayılan 08 ve 20) ve hastanenin saat dilimi. A
  nöbeti ilk başlangıçtan ikincisine, B nöbeti ikincisinden ertesi günün ilk başlangıcına kadardır (gece yarısını aşar ve
  başladığı güne aittir); sınır anı yeni nöbeti başlatır. İki başlangıç arasında 12 saat olmak zorunda değildir (08 ve
  16: 8 ve 16 saatlik nöbetler). «Bu nöbet» şu anı içeren, «Sonraki nöbet» ondan sonraki nöbettir. Sınırlar yerel
  saattir: yaz saati uygulanan bir dilimde (Bakü ve İstanbul uygulamaz) saatin geri alındığı gece B 13, ileri alındığı
  gece 11 saat sürer. Sıvı dengesinin günü (§3.17) bu dilimde değişmedi ve her zaman 24 saattir; ikisi yalnız o iki
  gece bir saat ayrışır.
- **Sayfanın sırası (yukarıdan aşağı):**
  1. **Başlık:** «İş yükü», nöbet («Bu nöbet · 08:00–20:00»), son tam okumanın saati («10:42 itibarıyla»), «Sayım, puan
     değil · kişiler sıralanmaz», «Yenile».
  2. **«Birimler · bu nöbet»:** her açık birim için bir satır. «Hasta»: dolu yatak (dolu, taburcu ya da nakil
     bekleyen). «Hemşire»: bu nöbet atanan hemşire sayısı; hiç atama yoksa «Bu nöbet için atama yok». «Hemşire başına
     hasta»: «3 hasta / 2 hemşire · ortalama 1,5» — hemşiresi olan dolu yataklar ÷ hemşire; hedef yok, renk yok.
     «Atanmamış hasta»: hemşiresi olmayan dolu yataklar. «Geciken iş»: sunucunun geç dediği açık el görevleri ve
     zamanı geçmiş vital ölçüm. «Kalan iş»: nöbet bitmeden zamanı gelen, henüz geç olmayan açık el görevleri (vital
     ölçüm dahil değil). «Yanıt bekleyen alarm»: canlı pano, yalnız pano canlıyken. Kapalı birim yazılmaz. Nöbet
     saatleri nöbetin ortasında değiştirilirse tablonun üstünde «Nöbet saatleri değişti; bu nöbetin ataması yeniden
     yapılmalı.» yazar: eski saatlerle yapılan atama yeni nöbete sayılmaz; o birimin bir sonraki kaydı onu kapatır.
  3. **«Hemşireler · {birim}»:** «Birim» seçici («Tüm birimler» ya da bir birim), «Alfabetik; sıralama değildir» ve
     seçilen birim(ler)de yatağı olan her hemşire için bir kart, ada göre: yatakları (yapıdaki adlarıyla, ör. «1 · Otaq
     1 · Reanimasiya»), hastaları (dolu yatakları) ve o hastaların «Kalan iş»i. Kartta geciken iş ve alarm sayısı yoktur
     (sahibin kararı, 2026-11-12: «Hayır, yalnız birimde»). Aynı adı taşıyan iki hemşire kullanıcı adıyla ayrılır.
     Sonra «Atanmamış»: hemşiresi olmayan dolu yataklar.
  4. **«Nöbet ataması · {birim}»** (yalnız `shifts.manage`): «Bu nöbet» / «Sonraki nöbet», nöbetin saatleri, birimin
     her açık yatağı için bir seçici («— atanmadı —» ve sunulan kişiler), «Kaydet». Sunulan kişiler: hastanenin
     varsayılan «Hemşire» ve «Sorumlu Hemşire» gruplarındaki etkin üyeler (Hızlı Yanıt Ekibi değil; hastanenin kendi
     hemşire grubu da değil). Kendi kapsamı bu birime ulaşmayan biri «bu birimi göremez» diye işaretlenir; atanabilir,
     ama kaydedince bir uyarı çıkar: kendi panosunda bu yatakların hastaları görünmez. Nöbetin ortasında hesabı ya da
     üyeliği kapanan biri tuttuğu yatağı koruyabilir, yeni yatak alamaz («artık atanamaz»). Kaydetmek o birimin o
     nöbetteki atamasını bütünüyle değiştirir; bırakılan atamalar silinmez, kapatıldıkları anla birlikte kapatılır.
     Form, yapı, nöbet ve kişi listesi okunamadıysa çizilmez, nedenini söyler.
- **Aynı anda iki kayıt, ve nöbet değişirken:** form okuduğu atamayı kaydederken sunucuya geri gönderir; arada başka
  biri kaydettiyse kayıt yapılmaz ve form «Bu arada başka biri değiştirdi — liste yenilendi; yeniden bakın.» der. Form
  19:50'de açılıp 20:00'den sonra kaydedilirse gösterdiği nöbet artık ne «bu» ne «sonraki» olabilir: kayıt yapılmaz,
  «Nöbet bu arada değişti — liste yenilendi; yeni nöbete yeniden bakın.» Başka bir red «Kaydedilmedi: {neden}» der,
  seçimler korunur.
- **Her sayı ya gerçektir ya da neden yok olduğunu söyler.** Okunurken, okunamadığında, reddedildiğinde, pano canlı
  değilken («pano güncel değil») ya da yanıtın nöbeti bitmişken («nöbet değişti, yenileniyor») sayının yerine «—» ve
  nedeni yazar; asla 0 yazılmaz. «Bu nöbet için atama yok» yalnız taze bir yanıt birimi atamasız gösterdiğinde yazılır;
  kullanıcının birim ataması dışındaki bir birimde (yalnız yatak ataması da birimi kapsamaz) «yetki yok» yazar.
- **Ne zaman okunur:** sayfa açılınca, «Yenile»de ve sayfa görünürken dakikada bir — yapı, kapsam, nöbet ataması ve
  görevler birlikte. Görevler nöbetin bitişinden geriye 7 gün okunur: nöbet değişirken zaten gecikmiş bir görev sayılır,
  yarının görevleri sayılmaz. Form kendi birimini ve nöbetini ayrıca okur; dakikalık okuma düzenlenmekte olan bir formu
  bozmaz.
- **Sunucuda:**
  - yeni `GET /shift-schedule?shift=current|next` (`workload.view` ya da `shifts.manage`): nöbet ve kapsamdaki her
    birim, bu nöbetin etkin atamalarıyla (bir satırın birimi yatağının birimidir); `GET /shift-schedule/nurses` ve
    `PUT /shift-schedule` (`shifts.manage`). Kayıt nöbeti başlangıç anıyla adlandırır; her kayıt denetim kaydına
    `shift.assign` olarak yazılır («Denetim bütünlüğü»nde «Nöbet ataması»; yalnız kimlikler). Hiçbir ekranın
    kullanmadığı eski `POST /shift-schedule` ve S-25'in «hemşire başına en çok yatak» uyarısı kaldırıldı (uyarı,
    kaynağı olmayan bir kod varsayılanına dayanıyordu);
  - göç `0022`: Sorumlu Hemşire gruplarına `workload.view`; tabloya kapanış anı (`closed_at`); yanlış birime yazılmış
    ve aynı yatak ile nöbet için birden fazla etkin olan eski satırlar kapatılır (en yenisi kalır); sonra bir yatak bir
    nöbette yalnız bir etkin hemşire tutar. Göç geri alınırsa kapatılan satırlar yeniden açılmaz.
- **Bu dilimde olmayanlar:** saat saat iş dağılımı; «Yeniden dağıtım önerisi»; puan, ölçek ya da hedef oran; atanan
  hemşirenin yatak kartında ya da hasta ekranında görünmesi; atamanın pano kapsamı, görev yönlendirmesi ya da alarm
  çağrısı için kullanılması; hastanenin kendi hemşire grupları; nöbet geçmişi ekranı. HBYS yatak eşlemesiyle yerleşen
  bir hastada yapının yatağı ile panonun yatağı ayrışabilir: kart «0» hasta gösterirken görevleri panodan gelir
  («Yönetici özeti»ndeki gibi, §3.24).
- **Açık:** Azerbaycanca, Rusça ve İngilizce metinler yerel okuma bekliyor.
- **Kaynak:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-12-g4-is-yuku-design.md` (§5 değişiklikleriyle); plan
  `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-12-g4-is-yuku.md`.
